+def test_01_rank1_hybrid_single_list_formatted_and_kind() -> None:
+    raw_results = {
+        "ids": ["a"],
+        "documents": ["doc a"],
+        "metadatas": [{}],
+        "distances": [0.5],
+        "scores": [0.5],
+        "search_type": "hybrid_rrf",
+    }
+    results = format_search_results(
+        raw_results,
+        collection_name="visa_oracle",
+        score_kind=score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED,
+    )
+    assert results[0]["score"] == 0.6667
+    assert results[0]["score_kind"] == score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED
+    assert results[0]["score_raw"] == 0.5
+

+def test_02_rank1_hybrid_both_lists_formatted_and_kind() -> None:
+    raw_results = {
+        "ids": ["a"],
+        "documents": ["doc a"],
+        "metadatas": [{}],
+        "distances": [0.0],
+        "scores": [1.0],
+        "search_type": "hybrid_rrf",
+    }
+    results = format_search_results(
+        raw_results,
+        collection_name="visa_oracle",
+        score_kind=score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED,
+    )
+    assert results[0]["score"] == 1.0
+    assert results[0]["score_kind"] == score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED
+    assert results[0]["score_raw"] == 1.0
+

+def test_03_rank5_hybrid_one_list_formatted_and_kind() -> None:
+    fusion = 0.166667  # MEASURED table row 7: single-list rank0=4, 1/(2+4)
+    raw_results = {
+        "ids": ["a"],
+        "documents": ["doc a"],
+        "metadatas": [{}],
+        "distances": [1.0 - fusion],
+        "scores": [fusion],
+        "search_type": "hybrid_rrf",
+    }
+    results = format_search_results(
+        raw_results,
+        collection_name="visa_oracle",
+        score_kind=score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED,
+    )
+    assert results[0]["score"] == 0.5455
+    assert results[0]["score_kind"] == score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED
+    assert results[0]["score_raw"] == fusion

+# ============================================================================

+def test_07_collection_boost_preserves_number_and_kind() -> None:
+    from backend.app.core.constants import SearchConstants
+
+    raw_results = {
+        "ids": ["a"],
+        "documents": ["doc a"],
+        "metadatas": [{}],

+        raw_results,
+        collection_name="bali_zero_pricing_hybrid",
+        score_kind=score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED,

+    base_score = 1 / (1 + 0.5)
+    expected = round(

+# ============================================================================
+# Case 9 — NEGATIVE, must fail on origin/main: a dense fallback whose
+# raw_results carries no `search_type` is labelled dense_formatted even

+# (checked via the key's PRESENCE, never its value), the search_type lie is

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_09_dense_fallback_without_search_type_key_labelled_dense_formatted(
+    search_service: SearchService,
+) -> None:
+    async def fake_hybrid_search(*, query_embedding, query_sparse, filter, limit, prefetch_limit):
+        # Simulates core/qdrant_db.py:1362's internal fallback: a
+        # dense-shaped dict that never sets "search_type" at all.

+            "ids": ["1"],
+            "documents": ["doc"],
+            "metadatas": [{}],
+            "distances": [0.1],
+            "scores": [0.9],
+            "total_found": 1,

+        mock_cache.set = AsyncMock()

+    # But score_kind tells the truth: this was a dense-shaped result.

+def _formatter_backed_retriever() -> FakeRetriever:
+    """A retriever whose hits are minted by the REAL formatter.
+
+    Case 10 is a CROSSING test, so its inputs may not be hand-written dicts
+    carrying a hand-written `score_kind`: that would assert the test's own
+    imagination rather than the chain. These hits are produced by calling
+    `format_search_results` exactly as `SearchService.hybrid_search` calls it
+    on the hybrid branch — the same function, the same declared kind — so what
+    the package builder receives here has the shape production hands it.
+
+    The first draft of case 10 used the shared `_visa_retriever()` fake, whose
+    hits declare no kind at all. It passed on key-presence alone while every
+    source reached the scorer as UNKNOWN, which is the precise failure the
+    strengthened assertion below exists to catch.
+    """
+    raw = {
+        "ids": ["1", "2"],
+        "documents": [
+            "KITAS requires a sponsor letter and passport copy.",
+            "Immigration law UU 6/2011 governs stay permits.",
+        ],
+        "metadatas": [{}, {}],
+        # 1/(2+0) and 1/(2+1): first and second of ONE contributing list,
+        # from the measured server-side RRF table.
+        "distances": [1.0 - 0.5, 1.0 - (1.0 / 3.0)],
+        "scores": [0.5, 1.0 / 3.0],
+        "total_found": 2,
+    }
+    formatted = format_search_results(
+        raw,
+        "visa_oracle",
+        primary_collection=None,
+        query=VISA_QUERY,
+        score_kind=score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED,
+    )
+    return FakeRetriever({"visa_oracle": formatted})
+
+
+# ============================================================================
+# Case 10 — PROJECTION test (staff-room requirement 2): the list handed to
+# calculate_evidence_score at wa_package_builder.py:594-598 carries
+# score_kind on EVERY element. Fails if the projection is ever re-narrowed
+# to {"score": ...} only.
+# ============================================================================
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_10_scorer_projection_carries_score_kind_on_every_element(
+    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
+) -> None:
+    captured: dict[str, list] = {}
+
+    def fake_calculate_evidence_score(*, sources, context_gathered, query):
+        captured["sources"] = sources
+        return 0.5
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(wpb_module, "calculate_evidence_score", fake_calculate_evidence_score)
+
+    await wpb_module.build_context_package(
+        query=VISA_QUERY,
+        history=[],
+        thread_epoch=0,
+        retriever=_formatter_backed_retriever(),
+    )
+
+    assert captured.get("sources"), "expected at least one source handed to the scorer"
+    for source in captured["sources"]:
+        assert score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY in source
+    # Presence alone is not enough: stamping every element UNKNOWN would also
+    # satisfy the loop above while destroying the contract's whole point. At
+    # least one element must carry a kind that a WRITER actually declared.
+    declared = {
+        source[score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY] for source in captured["sources"]
+    } - {score_provenance.UNKNOWN}
+    assert declared, (
+        "every source reached the scorer as UNKNOWN — the projection kept the key "
+        "but the chain lost the provenance somewhere upstream"
+    )
+
+

+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_11_real_package_prints_every_source_with_its_kind() -> None:

+        for i, chunk in enumerate(package.chunks)
+    ]
+    print("\n".join(["Real package sources (B1.1 crossing test #11):", *lines]))
+    assert package.chunks
+    for chunk in package.chunks:
+        assert chunk["score_kind"] in score_provenance.SCORE_KINDS

+# ============================================================================
+# Case 12 (addition, R2 backward-compat condition — staff-room ack
+# 2026-09-10T20:54:08Z, reiterated by the Dux): `package_hash` is
+# persisted and RE-VERIFIED ACROSS THE DEPLOY BOUNDARY
+# (services/integrations/wa_broker.py:468/691/885,
+# app/routers/wa_broker.py:279, app/routers/wa_package.py:161,
+# services/integrations/wa_codex_daemon.py:342) — so the question is not
+# "does the new hash differ" but "does a package sealed BEFORE this PR
+# still verify after it". `_canonical_wire`/`_package_hash` have no
+# per-field allowlist (spec R2): they are content-agnostic
+# `json.dumps(sort_keys=True)` over whatever they are given, so a stored
+# dict lacking score_kind/score_raw must re-hash to its own original value,
+# unchanged.
+# ============================================================================
+
+
+def test_a_package_sealed_before_score_kind_existed_still_verifies() -> None:
+    """A pre-PR sealed package — chunks with NO score_kind/score_raw key at
+    all — must still verify under the NEW code.
+
+    The proof is `ContextPackage.__post_init__` (wa_package_builder.py
+    :153-170) itself NOT raising on construction: it recomputes
+    `_canonical_wire` over the given fields and raises ValueError if the
+    digest doesn't match `package_hash`. That IS the real verification the
+    deploy boundary relies on when it reloads a stored package — this test
+    exercises that exact path rather than re-implementing the hash check.
+    """
+    history = [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}]
+    chunks = [{"collection": "visa_oracle", "text": "legacy chunk, no score_kind", "score": 0.7}]
+    assert score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY not in chunks[0]  # the pre-PR shape, made explicit
+    pricing_block = None
+    persona_digest = "pd"
+    evidence_inputs = {
+        "evidence_score": 0.5,
+        "context_length": 1,
+        "domain": "visa",
+        "label_threshold": 0.12,
+        "abstain": False,
+        "dlp": False,
+    }
+    thread_epoch = 1
+
+    # The digest a pre-PR build would have sealed — computed once, exactly
+    # as the original build did, never recomputed again below.
+    package_hash = wpb_module._package_hash(
+        history=history,
+        chunks=chunks,
+        pricing_block=pricing_block,
+        persona_digest=persona_digest,
+        evidence_inputs=evidence_inputs,
+        thread_epoch=thread_epoch,
+    )
+
+    # __post_init__ re-verifies digest == wire; constructing without a
+    # raised ValueError IS the proof that the pre-PR digest still holds.
+    package = ContextPackage(
+        history=history,
+        chunks=chunks,
+        pricing_block=pricing_block,
+        persona_digest=persona_digest,
+        evidence_inputs=evidence_inputs,
+        thread_epoch=thread_epoch,
+        package_hash=package_hash,
+    )
+    assert package.package_hash == package_hash

+# B1.1 build spec — the score contract, pinned end to end
+
+Base sha `1d726045c9`. Root for relative paths: `apps/backend-rag/backend/`.
+Every file:line below was re-read on this base; the vocabulary module
+`core/score_provenance.py` is ALREADY WRITTEN and is the contract's single home.
+
+## The two design rulings the implementer must not relitigate
+
+**R1 — provenance is DECLARED by the caller that knows, never derived from `search_type`.**
+`core/qdrant_db.py` emits `"search_type": "hybrid_rrf"` on the hybrid path (`:1338`, `:1373`,
+`:1389`) and emits NO `search_type` at all from the dense `search()` returns (`:519`, `:597`,
+`:608`), so `services/search/search_service.py:1142`'s
+`raw_results.get("search_type", "hybrid_rrf")` labels a dense fallback as hybrid. That is a real
+defect and B1 does NOT fix it: `search_type` is a served field (it is the label of the
+`rag_vector_search` metric family, `app/metrics.py:852`, and it is returned in the response and
+written to the cache), so changing its value is a served-behaviour change and belongs to B2. It is
+recorded as an inventory row and as a declared residual. Instead, `search_service` — which knows
+from its own branch which call it made — PASSES the kind down. A wrong `search_type` therefore can
+no longer make a wrong `score_kind`.
+
+**R2 — provenance lives INSIDE the sealed bytes, and the digest changes.**
+`_package_hash` (`services/rag/agentic/wa_package_builder.py:434-453`) hashes `chunks` as raw
+dicts with no per-field allowlist, and `_canonical_wire` (`:424-431`) is `json.dumps(sort_keys=True)`
+over whatever it is given, so a per-chunk `score_kind` enters both `package_hash` and `wire_text()`
+automatically. That is the objective, not a side effect: B1's §1 goal is that "the sealed package
+proves the scorer receives the same provenance whose bytes were sealed". `__post_init__`
+(`:153-170`) re-verifies hash==wire, so the two stay self-consistent by construction.
+**The digest therefore CHANGES, and the PR says so in plain words.** The STOP condition attached to
+it (`B1-design.md` §4(d), "a digest change with rows in flight is a STOP") is measured and NOT
+triggered: `wa_outbox` holds only terminal rows, 241 `failed` + 179 `done`, newest
+2026-09-01 09:27:53Z, zero `pending`/`generating` — receipt in `measured-fusion-table.md`.
+**`evidence_inputs` is NOT touched**, deliberately: `tests/unit/services/test_wa_codex_leg.py:159`
+and `:1302` pin its exact JSON substring in the wire, and B2 can read the kinds off the chunks.
+`context_length` is `len(chunks)` at `:601` (the file's only occurrence) and is key-agnostic, so it
+does not move.
+
+## The writers (additive only — no field renamed or removed, no numeric value changed)
+
+| #   | File:line                                                                                                              | Value today                                         | Declared kind                                            | `score_raw`                                                                          |
+| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
+| 1   | `services/misc/result_formatter.py:89` via `search_service.py:1158` (hybrid branch)                                    | `1/(1+distance)` over server RRF                    | `hybrid_rrf_formatted`                                   | the fusion output, i.e. `raw_results["scores"][i]`                                   |
+| 2   | same formatter via the dense branch (`search_service.py:1143-1154`) and the internal fallback `core/qdrant_db.py:1362` | `1/(1+distance)` over a cosine                      | `dense_formatted`                                        | the cosine, i.e. `raw_results["scores"][i]`                                          |
+| 3   | `core/reranker.py:169-180` and `services/rag/reranker.py:345-352`                                                      | reranker `relevance_score` overwrites `score`       | `reranked`                                               | the value it replaced (the same number those writers already keep as `vector_score`) |
+| 4   | `services/rag/agentic/wa_package_builder.py:529`                                                                       | literal `1.0`                                       | `curated_synthetic`                                      | `None` — nothing was consumed                                                        |
+| 5   | `services/search/search_service.py:1100-1103` (cache hit)                                                              | whatever was cached, pre-formatted                  | `unknown` when the entry predates this PR                | `None`                                                                               |

+
+| Path                | file:line                                                                | What it actually is                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

+## Crossing tests (new file `tests/unit/services/rag/agentic/test_score_contract_crossing.py`)
+
+Each asserts the EXACT numeric `score` and the `score_kind` that arrive at the scorer, crossing
+producer → formatter/reranker → source projection → package construction → DLP/capping →
+canonical serialization → scorer input:
+
+1. rank-1 hybrid, in ONE list → fusion `0.5` → formatted `0.6667`, kind `hybrid_rrf_formatted`.
+2. rank-1 hybrid, in BOTH lists → fusion `1.0` → formatted `1.0`, same kind.
+3. rank-5 hybrid (`rank0=4`) in one list → fusion `0.1667` → formatted `0.5455`, same kind.
+   (All three numbers are taken from the MEASURED table, not from a local re-derivation.)
+4. dense fallback at cosine `0.0` / `0.5` / `0.9` → formatted `0.5` / `0.6667` / `0.9091`,
+   kind `dense_formatted`.
+5. a reranked source → kind `reranked`, `score_raw` == the pre-rerank value == `vector_score`.
+6. curated synthetic `1.0` → kind `curated_synthetic`, `score_raw` absent.
+7. one collection boost applied → the boosted number is unchanged from today AND the kind survives
+   the boost (a boost does not change what the number IS).
+8. an `unknown` cache hit → a cached entry with no `score_kind` reads back as `unknown`.
+9. **NEGATIVE, must fail on origin/main:** a dense fallback whose `raw_results` carries no
+   `search_type` is labelled `dense_formatted` even though `search_service.py:1142` still calls it
+   `"hybrid_rrf"` — the two disagree ON PURPOSE and the test pins the disagreement, because the
+   kind comes from the caller and the `search_type` lie is B2's to fix.
+10. **PROJECTION test (staff-room requirement 2):** the list handed to
+    `calculate_evidence_score` at `wa_package_builder.py:594-598` carries `score_kind` on every
+    element. This is the test that fails if anyone re-narrows the projection to `{"score": ...}`.
+11. A printed real package from the fixture path shows every source with its kind (PR body).
+
+## Innocence
+
+The six files at their origin/main counts or better: `test_evidence_cross_language.py`,
+`test_evidence_scoring_abstain.py`, `test_wa_package_builder.py`, `test_wa_greeting.py`,
+`test_curated_qa_government_fee_gate.py`, `test_wa_finalize.py`. Plus the widest scorer suites,
+which this PR must not move: `tests/services/rag/agentic/test_reasoning_utils.py` (84),
+`tests/unit/services/rag/agentic/test_reasoning.py` (37),
+`tests/services/rag/test_confidence_scoring.py` (21). Any test that pins the WIRE or the
+`package_hash` by literal value is expected to move, and every such move is listed in the PR body
+with the reason "digest change, declared, no rows in flight".

+## The sibling artifact B1.2 consumes — and the reason it cannot be adopted as it stands
+
+The branch carries **three** unpushed commits, and the pack cites all three (the first draft of
+this inventory recorded only the first — the staff room caught it, and the correction matters
+because the second commit is a design question, not a duplicate):
+
+| sha                                        | subject                                                                          | what it is                                         |
+| ------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
+| `92f40801235cf33b32a8d71f241b6400cad64536` | test(bot): rebase nine abstain tripwires on measured embedding cosines           | the rebase + `measured_cosines.py`                 |
+| `30a0205dab`                               | test(bot): guard abstain fixtures against unmeasured cosines                     | a 192-line guard, `test_measured_cosines_guard.py` |
+| `84bf272a24`                               | docs(evidence): brief for the nine abstain tripwires rebased on measured cosines | the lane's brief; branch tip                       |
+
+A local keep-ref `refs/keep/rc1a-2026-09-11 -> 84bf272a24` protects all three from a broker reap or
+a graveyard sweep. **Read the table by sha** (`git show 92f4080123:apps/backend-rag/backend/tests/fixtures/measured_cosines.py`),
+never from the working tree.
+
+### The guard commit inherits the same false premise, and B1's own vocabulary is what refutes it
+
+`30a0205dab`'s message says it "flagged exactly the seven >= 0.8 fixtures of the nine and nothing
+else in backend/tests" — that is a guard that judges a score by its MAGNITUDE, on the assumption
+that a high number is an implausible cosine. Under the measured pipeline a high number is entirely
+plausible: a result ranked first in BOTH prefetch lists fuses to 1.0 and formats to 1.0, and the
+curated block is minted at a literal 1.0. So that guard would flag legitimate pipeline values while
+passing a fictional 0.4. `core/score_provenance.py`'s first rule already states the principle that
+refutes it — a kind is declared by the writer that mints the value and is NEVER inferred from
+magnitude, because magnitudes overlap across kinds by construction.
+
+**B1.2 keeps the guard's INTENT and inverts its test.** The guard B1.2 ships is the one `B1-design.md`
+§4 specifies: it fails if a tripwire fixture declares a source `score` without a `score_kind` from
+the frozen vocabulary. Provenance-based, not magnitude-based — it cannot be fooled by a plausible
+number and cannot punish an implausible-looking true one.
+
+### The rebase commit
+
+Commit `92f40801235cf33b32a8d71f241b6400cad64536` (worktree
+`.worktrees/backend-rag-rc1a-fixtures-real-cosines`, branch
+`agent/nuzantara/backend-rag/rc1a-fixtures-real-cosines`, **never pushed**, broker lease ORPHAN)
+rebases these same nine onto real `text-embedding-3-small` cosines measured in one API request
+(20 texts, 216 prompt tokens) inside the prod `rag` container at 2026-09-10T18:03Z.
+
+Its own docstring states the premise the whole commit rests on: _"A `sources[].score` in a
+scorer fixture stands for the cosine the retriever would return for (query, retrieved chunk)."_
+**That premise is false.** `sources[].score` is what `wa_package_builder.py:289` forwards out of
+`result_formatter.py:89` — `1/(1+distance)` over a server-side RRF output on the hybrid path,
+`1/(2-cos)` on the dense path. It is never a cosine on either. Its measured values span 0.04-0.51
+and the measured live floor is 0.5082 hybrid / 0.5 dense, so **every one of them is below the
+floor**: as impossible as the 0.35-0.91 they replace, in the other direction.
+
+Two further divergences from D3/D5: the commit MOVES the inputs in place ("only the fictional
+inputs moved") where D3 requires the originals PRESERVED with variants ADDED, and its table is
+keyed on `(query, context)` text pairs so it cannot express contributing ranks, list membership or
+fusion configuration — the fields D3 makes mandatory.
+
+**What B1.2 does with it.** The cosines are sound as `score_raw` on the DENSE path, which is the
+one path with a real cosine behind the number, and the correct fixture value is one transform away:
+
+| measured cosine | → `score` = 1/(2−cos) |
+| --------------- | --------------------- |
+| 0.04            | 0.5102                |
+| 0.14            | 0.5376                |
+| 0.16            | 0.5435                |
+| 0.30            | 0.5882                |
+| 0.51            | 0.6711                |
+
+All five land above the 0.5082 hybrid floor, inside the live band. B1.2 therefore ADDS variants
+that carry the measured cosine as `score_raw`, the derived value as `score`, and
+`score_kind = dense_formatted`, beside the nine preserved originals — and makes **no new embedding
+call**, so D3's "no embedding API call is needed" holds without reopening any allowance.

+# B1.1 first artifact — the MEASURED rank ↔ returned-score table
+
+Bound to git base sha `1d726045c9ca75ab64a75e92b062e52b0b763a98`. Every citation below is a
+citation into THAT tree, never into a Fly release: prod moved from v4389 (the release live at
+measurement time) to v4396 at 20:47Z on unrelated compliance-stack deploys while this artifact
+was being written, which is exactly why the binding is to the git base.
+
+## Why this had to be measured
+
+`README.md` §0 states the fusion table as "an expectation, not a measurement": production fusion
+is server-side (`apps/backend-rag/backend/core/qdrant_db.py:1308`, `{"fusion": "rrf"}`) and the
+server's ranking constant is not verified from any file in this repo. The two in-repo RRF helpers
+(`backend/services/rag/hybrid_search.py:32`, `RRF_K = 60`, 1-based ranks; and
+`backend/services/search/search_service.py:897-916`) are NOT the hot path. The installed client's
+local-mode constant (`apps/backend-rag/.venv/lib/python3.11/site-packages/qdrant_client/hybrid/fusion.py:4-11`,
+`ranking_constant = 2`) is the package's file, not the server's behaviour.
+
+## Method
+
+Read-only, inside the prod container, machine `1781e5eda03438`.
+
+- The query vectors are **one existing point's own stored `dense` and `bm25` vectors**, fetched by
+  `points/scroll` with `with_vector=true`. **No embedding call was made.**
+- **No write** of any kind: only `GET /`, `GET /collections`, `GET /collections/{c}`,
+  `POST .../points/scroll` and `POST .../points/query`.
+- **Nothing was exported** except server version, collection name, point count, vector dimensions,
+  per-list ranks, list membership and returned scores. No payload, no point id, no vector, no
+  credential. The probe reads `QDRANT_URL`/`QDRANT_API_KEY` from the container environment and
+  never prints them.
+- The fused call is byte-shaped on the production payload at `core/qdrant_db.py:1288-1312`
+  (a `dense` + `bm25` prefetch under `{"fusion": "rrf"}`), with `prefetch_limit = limit * 3` as
+  `backend/services/search/search_service.py:1140` computes it.
+- The probe was uploaded to the container's `/tmp` with `fly ssh sftp put`, executed once with
+  `fly ssh console -C`, and deleted in a second `fly ssh console -C` call.
+
+This is the ONE prod-Qdrant read `B1-design.md` §2 grants B1 (the RC2 fence is write/export).
+Any further prod read is gated on a staff-room ack before it runs.
+
+## Measured
+
+- **Prod Qdrant server version `1.16.3`.** Pro's local Qdrant is `1.12.5`, so the local instance
+  was NOT usable as a stand-in — the version that decides the constant is the prod one.
+- Collection `kbli_2025_final_hybrid`, 1873 points, dense dim 1536, query sparse nnz 393.
+- `prefetch_limit = 30`, fused `limit = 10`.
+
+| fused_rank | dense_rank0 | bm25_rank0 | lists      | returned_score | distance | formatted |
+| ---------- | ----------- | ---------- | ---------- | -------------- | -------- | --------- |
+| 0          | 0           | 0          | dense+bm25 | 1.000000       | 0.000000 | 1.000000  |
+| 1          | 1           | 1          | dense+bm25 | 0.666667       | 0.333333 | 0.750000  |
+| 2          | 2           | 14         | dense+bm25 | 0.312500       | 0.687500 | 0.592593  |
+| 3          | 16          | 2          | dense+bm25 | 0.305556       | 0.694444 | 0.590164  |
+| 4          | 7           | 4          | dense+bm25 | 0.277778       | 0.722222 | 0.580645  |
+| 5          | 20          | 3          | dense+bm25 | 0.245455       | 0.754545 | 0.569948  |
+| 6          | 3           | —          | dense      | 0.200000       | 0.800000 | 0.555556  |
+| 7          | 4           | —          | dense      | 0.166667       | 0.833333 | 0.545455  |
+| 8          | 5           | —          | dense      | 0.142857       | 0.857143 | 0.538462  |
+| 9          | —           | 5          | bm25       | 0.142857       | 0.857143 | 0.538462  |
+
+`distance` is `1.0 - returned_score` (`core/qdrant_db.py:1335`); `formatted` is `1/(1+distance)`
+(`backend/services/misc/result_formatter.py:89`), shown WITHOUT any collection boost.
+
+## Verdict
+
+**`returned_score = Σ over contributing lists of 1 / (2 + rank0)`** — zero-based rank, ranking
+constant 2. Exact on all ten rows (`MATCHES_k2_zero_based=True`). The k=60 / 1-based hypothesis is
+refuted on all ten (`MATCHES_k60_one_based=False`).
+
+So the SERVER's constant equals the installed client's local-mode constant. The README's
+expectation table is CONFIRMED by measurement rather than assumed; there is no reconciliation gap
+to declare, and B1.1(c)'s values derive from a table that is now both measured and local. Kimi K1's
+substantive point holds: the in-repo RRF helpers are not the hot path.
+
+## The consequence — finding F-B1.1a
+
+Fusion output is bounded BELOW at `1/(2 + prefetch_limit - 1) = 1/31 = 0.0323`, so
+`distance ≤ 0.9677` and `result_formatter.py:89` maps **every** hybrid result into
+**[0.508, 1.000]** before any collection boost. The dense fallback is bounded the same way for any
+non-negative cosine: `1/(2 - cos) ≥ 0.5` at `cos = 0`. The curated block is minted at a synthetic
+`1.0` (`services/rag/agentic/wa_package_builder.py:529`).
+
+Therefore `services/rag/agentic/reasoning_utils.py:665` —
+
+```
+if 0 < top_source_cosine < 0.5 and final_score > 0.15:
+```
+
+— **cannot fire on any live path**: not hybrid, not dense-fallback-with-non-negative-cosine, not
+curated. The only input that reaches it is a NEGATIVE cosine (which makes `distance > 1`), and
+1536-dim `text-embedding-3-small` vectors essentially never produce one. The comment above it at
+`:650` calls the field "Qdrant's per-source cosine similarity"; it is a ranking transform that
+structurally cannot enter the branch written to consume it.
+
+**The semantic-alignment penalty is effectively dead code in production.** That is now a measured
+claim, not a reading, and it sharpens D1 in a way the packet should carry: repairing provenance
+does not repair the scorer, and B2 will be inverting a metric one of whose two guards has never run.
+
+It also constrains B1.4: **no support-signal candidate may lean on `top_source_cosine` as currently
+minted**, because that number is not observable as a relevance signal on the live path.
+
+## Digest-compatibility receipt (B1.1(d) input)
+
+Measured read-only on prod Postgres via `scripts/pg.sh` at the same sitting:
+
+```
+ status | count |            newest
+--------+-------+-------------------------------
+ failed |   241 | 2026-09-01 08:31:11.591666+00
+ done   |   179 | 2026-09-01 09:27:53.327524+00
+```
+
+Only terminal statuses exist; there is no `pending` or `generating` row, and the newest row of any
+kind is 2026-09-01 09:27:53Z — the same instant Fable measured independently. **No package is in
+flight**, so the "a digest change with rows in flight is a STOP" condition of `B1-design.md` §4(d)
+is not triggered. This is a receipt about in-flight rows only; it is not a licence to change the
+digest casually, and the PR states the digest's actual disposition separately.

     1	# B1.1 build spec — the score contract, pinned end to end
     2	
     3	Base sha `1d726045c9`. Root for relative paths: `apps/backend-rag/backend/`.
     4	Every file:line below was re-read on this base; the vocabulary module
     5	`core/score_provenance.py` is ALREADY WRITTEN and is the contract's single home.
     6	
     7	## The two design rulings the implementer must not relitigate
     8	
     9	**R1 — provenance is DECLARED by the caller that knows, never derived from `search_type`.**
    10	`core/qdrant_db.py` emits `"search_type": "hybrid_rrf"` on the hybrid path (`:1338`, `:1373`,
    11	`:1389`) and emits NO `search_type` at all from the dense `search()` returns (`:519`, `:597`,
    12	`:608`), so `services/search/search_service.py:1142`'s
    13	`raw_results.get("search_type", "hybrid_rrf")` labels a dense fallback as hybrid. That is a real
    14	defect and B1 does NOT fix it: `search_type` is a served field (it is the label of the
    15	`rag_vector_search` metric family, `app/metrics.py:852`, and it is returned in the response and
    16	written to the cache), so changing its value is a served-behaviour change and belongs to B2. It is
    17	recorded as an inventory row and as a declared residual. Instead, `search_service` — which knows
    18	from its own branch which call it made — PASSES the kind down. A wrong `search_type` therefore can
    19	no longer make a wrong `score_kind`.
    20	
    21	**R2 — provenance lives INSIDE the sealed bytes, and the digest changes.**
    22	`_package_hash` (`services/rag/agentic/wa_package_builder.py:434-453`) hashes `chunks` as raw
    23	dicts with no per-field allowlist, and `_canonical_wire` (`:424-431`) is `json.dumps(sort_keys=True)`
    24	over whatever it is given, so a per-chunk `score_kind` enters both `package_hash` and `wire_text()`
    25	automatically. That is the objective, not a side effect: B1's §1 goal is that "the sealed package
    26	proves the scorer receives the same provenance whose bytes were sealed". `__post_init__`
    27	(`:153-170`) re-verifies hash==wire, so the two stay self-consistent by construction.
    28	**The digest therefore CHANGES, and the PR says so in plain words.** The STOP condition attached to
    29	it (`B1-design.md` §4(d), "a digest change with rows in flight is a STOP") is measured and NOT
    30	triggered: `wa_outbox` holds only terminal rows, 241 `failed` + 179 `done`, newest
    31	2026-09-01 09:27:53Z, zero `pending`/`generating` — receipt in `measured-fusion-table.md`.
    32	**`evidence_inputs` is NOT touched**, deliberately: `tests/unit/services/test_wa_codex_leg.py:159`
    33	and `:1302` pin its exact JSON substring in the wire, and B2 can read the kinds off the chunks.
    34	`context_length` is `len(chunks)` at `:601` (the file's only occurrence) and is key-agnostic, so it
    35	does not move.

    57	## Crossing tests (new file `tests/unit/services/rag/agentic/test_score_contract_crossing.py`)
    58	
    59	Each asserts the EXACT numeric `score` and the `score_kind` that arrive at the scorer, crossing
    60	producer → formatter/reranker → source projection → package construction → DLP/capping →
    61	canonical serialization → scorer input:
    62	
    63	1. rank-1 hybrid, in ONE list → fusion `0.5` → formatted `0.6667`, kind `hybrid_rrf_formatted`.
    64	2. rank-1 hybrid, in BOTH lists → fusion `1.0` → formatted `1.0`, same kind.
    65	3. rank-5 hybrid (`rank0=4`) in one list → fusion `0.1667` → formatted `0.5455`, same kind.
    66	   (All three numbers are taken from the MEASURED table, not from a local re-derivation.)
    67	4. dense fallback at cosine `0.0` / `0.5` / `0.9` → formatted `0.5` / `0.6667` / `0.9091`,
    68	   kind `dense_formatted`.
    69	5. a reranked source → kind `reranked`, `score_raw` == the pre-rerank value == `vector_score`.
    70	6. curated synthetic `1.0` → kind `curated_synthetic`, `score_raw` absent.
    71	7. one collection boost applied → the boosted number is unchanged from today AND the kind survives
    72	   the boost (a boost does not change what the number IS).
    73	8. an `unknown` cache hit → a cached entry with no `score_kind` reads back as `unknown`.
    74	9. **NEGATIVE, must fail on origin/main:** a dense fallback whose `raw_results` carries no
    75	   `search_type` is labelled `dense_formatted` even though `search_service.py:1142` still calls it
    76	   `"hybrid_rrf"` — the two disagree ON PURPOSE and the test pins the disagreement, because the
    77	   kind comes from the caller and the `search_type` lie is B2's to fix.
    78	10. **PROJECTION test (staff-room requirement 2):** the list handed to
    79	    `calculate_evidence_score` at `wa_package_builder.py:594-598` carries `score_kind` on every
    80	    element. This is the test that fails if anyone re-narrows the projection to `{"score": ...}`.
    81	11. A printed real package from the fixture path shows every source with its kind (PR body).

  1053	    async def hybrid_search(
  1054	        self,
  1055	        query: str,
  1056	        user_level: int,
  1057	        limit: int = 5,
  1058	        tier_filter: list[TierLevel] = None,
  1059	        collection_override: str | None = None,
  1060	        apply_filters: bool | None = None,
  1061	        fallback_to_plain: bool = True,
  1062	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1063	        """
  1064	        Hybrid search combining dense vectors and BM25 sparse vectors.
  1065	
  1066	        Uses Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF) to combine results from both
  1067	        dense semantic search and BM25 keyword search for optimal retrieval.
  1068	
  1069	        Results are cached in Redis (TTL 1h) keyed by query+user_level+limit+collection.
  1070	        Cache reduces avg latency from ~894ms to <100ms for repeated queries.
  1071	
  1072	        Args:
  1073	            query: Search query
  1074	            user_level: User access level (0-3)
  1075	            limit: Max results
  1076	            tier_filter: Optional specific tier filter
  1077	            collection_override: Force specific collection
  1078	            apply_filters: If True, apply tier/exclude_repealed filters
  1079	            fallback_to_plain: If True (default, historical behavior), any
  1080	                error in the hybrid path falls back to `self.search()` —
  1081	                which runs LLM query expansion (Gemini, via QueryExpander).
  1082	                Pass False on paths with a zero-LLM contract (the WA
  1083	                codex-route package builder, BOT-V4 S2): the error is
  1084	                re-raised so the caller degrades WITHOUT an LLM ever being
  1085	                reachable from this call.
  1086	
  1087	        Returns:
  1088	            Search results with hybrid search metadata
  1089	        """
  1090	        # --- Cache lookup ---
  1091	        from backend.core.cache import get_cache_service
  1092	
  1093	        cache = get_cache_service()
  1094	        cache_key = cache._generate_key(
  1095	            "hybrid_search",
  1096	            query,
  1097	            user_level=user_level,
  1098	            limit=limit,
  1099	            collection_override=collection_override or "",
  1100	        )
  1101	        cached_result = await cache.get(cache_key)
  1102	        if cached_result is not None:
  1103	            cached_result["cache_hit"] = True
  1104	            # B1.1: a cached entry written before score_provenance existed
  1105	            # (or by a legacy code path) carries no score_kind — declare it
  1106	            # UNKNOWN rather than leaving the field absent. No number moves.
  1107	            for entry in cached_result.get("results", []):
  1108	                if (
  1109	                    isinstance(entry, dict)
  1110	                    and score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY not in entry
  1111	                ):
  1112	                    score_provenance.stamp(entry, score_provenance.UNKNOWN)
  1113	            return cached_result
  1114	
  1115	        try:
  1116	            # Prepare search context (same as regular search)
  1117	            embedding_start = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1118	            (
  1119	                query_embedding,
  1120	                collection_name,
  1121	                vector_db,
  1122	                chroma_filter,
  1123	                _tier_values,
  1124	            ) = await self._prepare_search_context(
  1125	                query,
  1126	                user_level,
  1127	                tier_filter,
  1128	                collection_override,
  1129	                apply_filters,
  1130	            )
  1131	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE and embedding_start:
  1132	                rag_embedding_duration.observe(time.time() - embedding_start)
  1133	
  1134	            # Generate BM25 sparse vector
  1135	            query_sparse = None
  1136	            if self._bm25_vectorizer:
  1137	                query_sparse = self._bm25_vectorizer.generate_query_sparse_vector(query)
  1138	                logger.debug(
  1139	                    f"Generated BM25 sparse vector: {len(query_sparse.get('indices', []))} tokens",
  1140	                )
  1141	
  1142	            # Try hybrid search if available
  1143	            search_start = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1144	            if query_sparse and hasattr(vector_db, "hybrid_search"):
  1145	                raw_results = await vector_db.hybrid_search(
  1146	                    query_embedding=query_embedding,
  1147	                    query_sparse=query_sparse,
  1148	                    filter=chroma_filter,
  1149	                    limit=limit,
  1150	                    prefetch_limit=limit * 3,  # Get more candidates for fusion
  1151	                )
  1152	                search_type = raw_results.get("search_type", "hybrid_rrf")
  1153	                # B1.1 / ruling R1: score_kind is DECLARED by this branch,
  1154	                # never derived from the (known-lossy) search_type default
  1155	                # above. `vector_db.hybrid_search()` stamps its own native
  1156	                # RRF-shaped dict with a "search_type" key on every path
  1157	                # (success and its own empty-result error path) — the ONE
  1158	                # case that key is absent is when it silently degraded to
  1159	                # `self.search()` internally (core/qdrant_db.py:1362),
  1160	                # whose dense-shaped dict never carries that key. Checking
  1161	                # the key's PRESENCE (not its value) tells hybrid from
  1162	                # dense here without touching `search_type` itself.
  1163	                declared_kind = (
  1164	                    score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED
  1165	                    if "search_type" in raw_results
  1166	                    else score_provenance.DENSE_FORMATTED
  1167	                )
  1168	            else:
  1169	                # Fallback to dense-only search
  1170	                use_vector_name = "dense" if _uses_named_vectors(collection_name) else None
  1171	                raw_results = await vector_db.search(
  1172	                    query_embedding=query_embedding,
  1173	                    filter=chroma_filter,
  1174	                    limit=limit,
  1175	                    vector_name=use_vector_name,
  1176	                )
  1177	                search_type = "dense_only"
  1178	                declared_kind = score_provenance.DENSE_FORMATTED
  1179	
  1180	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE and search_start:
  1181	                rag_vector_search_duration.observe(time.time() - search_start)
  1182	
  1183	            # Format results
  1184	            formatted_results = format_search_results(
  1185	                raw_results,
  1186	                collection_name,
  1187	                primary_collection=None,
  1188	                query=query,
  1189	                score_kind=declared_kind,
  1190	            )
  1191	
  1192	            # Record query for health monitoring
  1193	            avg_score = (
  1194	                sum(r["score"] for r in formatted_results) / len(formatted_results)
  1195	                if formatted_results
  1196	                else 0.0
  1197	            )
  1198	            self.health_monitor.record_query(
  1199	                collection_name=collection_name,
  1200	                had_results=len(formatted_results) > 0,
  1201	                result_count=len(formatted_results),
  1202	                avg_score=avg_score,
  1203	            )
  1204	
  1205	            result = {
  1206	                "query": query,
  1207	                "results": formatted_results,
  1208	                "collection": collection_name,
  1209	                "total_results": len(formatted_results),
  1210	                "search_type": search_type,
  1211	                "bm25_enabled": query_sparse is not None,
  1212	            }
  1213	
  1214	            # --- Cache write (non-blocking, TTL from redis_manager config) ---
  1215	            if formatted_results:
  1216	                try:
  1217	                    await cache.set(cache_key, result)
  1218	                except Exception as cache_err:
  1219	                    logger.debug("Cache write failed (non-critical): %s", cache_err)
  1220	
  1221	            return result
  1222	
  1223	        except Exception as e:
  1224	            logger.error("Hybrid search error: %s", e, exc_info=True)
  1225	            if not fallback_to_plain:
  1226	                # Zero-LLM contract path (BOT-V4 S2): `self.search()` runs
  1227	                # Gemini query expansion, so falling back here would put an
  1228	                # LLM call on a path that promised none. Re-raise instead —
  1229	                # the caller owns the degradation.
  1230	                raise
  1231	            # Fallback to regular search on error
  1232	            return await self.search(
  1233	                query=query,
  1234	                user_level=user_level,
  1235	                limit=limit,
  1236	                tier_filter=tier_filter,
  1237	                collection_override=collection_override,
  1238	                apply_filters=apply_filters,
  1239	            )
  1240	
  1241	    async def hybrid_search_with_reranking(
  1242	        self,
  1243	        query: str,
  1244	        user_level: int,
  1245	        limit: int = 5,
  1246	        tier_filter: list[TierLevel] = None,
  1247	        collection_override: str | None = None,
  1248	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1249	        """
  1250	        Full hybrid search pipeline: BM25 + Dense + RRF + reranking.
  1251	
  1252	        This is the most comprehensive search method combining:
  1253	        1. Dense vector search (semantic)
  1254	        2. BM25 sparse vector search (keyword)
  1255	        3. Reciprocal Rank Fusion (combining both)
  1256	        4. CrossEncoder or Ze-Rank reranking (final precision)
  1257	
  1258	        Args:
  1259	            query: Search query
  1260	            user_level: User access level (0-3)
  1261	            limit: Max results (final count after all stages)
  1262	            tier_filter: Optional specific tier filter
  1263	            collection_override: Force specific collection
  1264	
  1265	        Returns:
  1266	            Search results with full pipeline metadata
  1267	        """
  1268	        pipeline_start_time = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1269	
  1270	        # 1. Hybrid search with overfetch for reranking
  1271	        initial_limit = limit * 3
  1272	        results = await self.hybrid_search(
  1273	            query=query,
  1274	            user_level=user_level,
  1275	            limit=initial_limit,
  1276	            tier_filter=tier_filter,
  1277	            collection_override=collection_override,
  1278	            apply_filters=True,
  1279	        )
  1280	
  1281	        # 2. Early exit for high-confidence results
  1282	        if results["results"] and results["results"][0].get("score", 0) > 0.85:
  1283	            logger.info(
  1284	                f"⚡ Early exit: Top result score {results['results'][0]['score']:.3f} > 0.85",
  1285	            )
  1286	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE:
  1287	                rag_early_exit_total.inc()
  1288	            results["results"] = results["results"][:limit]
  1289	            results["reranked"] = False
  1290	            results["early_exit"] = True
  1291	            return results
  1292	
  1293	        # 3. Re-rank with Ze-Rank 2
  1294	        reranker = self._init_reranker()
  1295	        if reranker.enabled:
  1296	            logger.info(
  1297	                f"🔍 Re-ranking {len(results['results'])} hybrid candidates for: '{query[:50]}'",
  1298	            )
  1299	            rerank_start = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1300	            reranked_docs = await reranker.rerank(query, results["results"], top_k=limit)
  1301	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE and rerank_start:
  1302	                rag_reranking_duration.observe(time.time() - rerank_start)
  1303	            results["results"] = reranked_docs
  1304	            results["reranked"] = True
  1305	            results["early_exit"] = False
  1306	        else:
  1307	            results["results"] = results["results"][:limit]
  1308	            results["reranked"] = False
  1309	            results["early_exit"] = False
  1310

   924	    async def get(self, ids: list[str], include: list[str] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   925	        """
   926	        Retrieve points by IDs (Qdrant-compatible interface).
   927	
   928	        Args:
   929	            ids: List of point IDs to retrieve
   930	            include: List of fields to include (e.g., ["embeddings", "payload"])
   931	
   932	        Returns:
   933	            Dictionary with Qdrant-compatible format
   934	        """
   935	        try:
   936	            client = await self._get_client()
   937	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points"
   938	
   939	            # Qdrant retrieve endpoint (POST /collections/{name}/points).
   940	            # BUG FIX (2026-07-19, verified live task #22): with_payload/with_vector
   941	            # are JSON BODY fields on this endpoint, NOT query params — Qdrant's
   942	            # REST API silently ignores them when sent as query params, so any
   943	            # caller passing include=[...] got 200 OK with the flags dropped
   944	            # (empty payload/vectors, scar family #2 "green but not working").
   945	            # Ref: https://api.qdrant.tech/api-reference/points/get-points
   946	            # (PointRequest schema: ids / with_payload / with_vector — singular
   947	            # "with_vector", not "with_vectors").
   948	            payload: dict[str, Any] = {"ids": ids}
   949	            if include:
   950	                # Map Qdrant-compatible include to Qdrant with_payload/with_vector
   951	                with_payload = "payload" in include or "metadatas" in include
   952	                with_vector = "embeddings" in include
   953	                if with_payload:
   954	                    payload["with_payload"] = True
   955	                if with_vector:
   956	                    payload["with_vector"] = True
   957	            else:
   958	                payload["with_payload"] = True
   959	                payload["with_vector"] = True
   960	
   961	            try:
   962	                response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
   963	                response.raise_for_status()
   964	
   965	                results = response.json().get("result", [])
   966	
   967	                # Transform to Qdrant format
   968	                formatted = {"ids": [], "embeddings": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
   969	
   970	                for point in results:
   971	                    formatted["ids"].append(str(point["id"]))
   972	                    if "vector" in point:
   973	                        formatted["embeddings"].append(point["vector"])
   974	                    else:
   975	                        formatted["embeddings"].append(None)
   976	
   977	                    payload_data = point.get("payload", {})
   978	                    formatted["documents"].append(payload_data.get("text", ""))
   979	                    formatted["metadatas"].append(_extract_point_metadata(payload_data))
   980	
   981	                return formatted
   982	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
   983	                logger.error(f"Qdrant get failed: {e.response.status_code} - {e.response.text}")
   984	                return {"ids": [], "embeddings": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
   985	            except Exception as e:
   986	                logger.error("Qdrant get request error: %s", e)
   987	                return {"ids": [], "embeddings": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
   988	
   989	        except Exception as e:
   990	            logger.error("Qdrant get error: %s", e)
   991	            return {"ids": [], "embeddings": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
   992	
   993	    async def set_payload(self, ids: list[str], payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   994	        """Merge payload fields into existing points without replacing provenance."""
   995	        if not ids:
   996	            raise ValueError("ids cannot be empty")
   997	        if not payload:
   998	            raise ValueError("payload cannot be empty")
   999	
  1000	        client = await self._get_client()
  1001	        response = await client.post(
  1002	            f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/payload",
  1003	            json={"points": ids, "payload": payload},
  1004	            params={"wait": "true"},
  1005	        )
  1006	        response.raise_for_status()
  1007	        return {
  1008	            "success": True,
  1009	            "updated": len(ids),
  1010	            "collection": self.collection_name,
  1011	        }

  1013	    async def set_payload_by_filter(
  1014	        self,
  1015	        *,
  1016	        metadata_filter: dict[str, Any],
  1017	        payload: dict[str, Any],
  1018	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1019	        """Atomically merge payload into every point matching a strict filter."""
  1020	        if not metadata_filter:
  1021	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter cannot be empty")
  1022	        if not payload:
  1023	            raise ValueError("payload cannot be empty")
  1024	        qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1025	            metadata_filter,
  1026	            include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1027	        )
  1028	        if not qdrant_filter:
  1029	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter did not produce a Qdrant filter")
  1030	
  1031	        client = await self._get_client()
  1032	        response = await client.post(
  1033	            f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/payload",
  1034	            json={"filter": qdrant_filter, "payload": payload},
  1035	            params={"wait": "true"},
  1036	        )
  1037	        response.raise_for_status()
  1038	        return {"success": True, "collection": self.collection_name}

  1040	    async def delete_by_filter(
  1041	        self,
  1042	        *,
  1043	        metadata_filter: dict[str, Any],
  1044	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1045	        """Delete every point matching a strict metadata filter or raise."""
  1046	        if not metadata_filter:
  1047	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter cannot be empty")
  1048	        qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1049	            metadata_filter,
  1050	            include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1051	        )
  1052	        if not qdrant_filter:
  1053	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter did not produce a Qdrant filter")
  1054	
  1055	        client = await self._get_client()
  1056	        response = await client.post(
  1057	            f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/delete",
  1058	            json={"filter": qdrant_filter},
  1059	            params={"wait": "true"},
  1060	        )
  1061	        response.raise_for_status()
  1062	        return {"success": True, "collection": self.collection_name}

  1064	    async def delete(
  1065	        self,
  1066	        ids: list[str],
  1067	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1068	        """
  1069	        Delete points by IDs (Qdrant-compatible interface).
  1070	
  1071	        Args:
  1072	            ids: List of point IDs to delete
  1073	
  1074	        Returns:
  1075	            Dictionary with operation results
  1076	
  1077	        Raises:

  1080	        try:

  1082	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/delete"
  1083	
  1084	            payload = {"points": ids}

  1087	                response.raise_for_status()

  1091	                )

  1093	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
  1094	                logger.error(f"Qdrant delete failed: {e.response.status_code} - {e.response.text}")

  1096	            except Exception as e:

  1098	                raise QdrantConnectionError(f"Qdrant connection error: {e}") from e

  1114	    async def scroll(
  1115	        self,
  1116	        limit: int = 10,
  1117	        metadata_filter: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  1118	    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1119	        """
  1120	        Scroll through points in the collection with optional filtering.
  1121	
  1122	        Args:
  1123	            limit: Maximum number of points to return
  1124	            metadata_filter: Optional filter dict (e.g., {"agent": "main"})
  1125	
  1126	        Returns:
  1127	            List of points with id and payload: [{"id": "...", "payload": {...}}, ...]
  1128	        """
  1129	        try:
  1130	            client = await self._get_client()
  1131	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/scroll"
  1132	
  1133	            payload = {"limit": limit, "with_payload": True, "with_vectors": False}
  1134	
  1135	            # Add filter if provided
  1136	            if metadata_filter:
  1137	                qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1138	                    metadata_filter,
  1139	                    include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1140	                )
  1141	                if qdrant_filter:
  1142	                    payload["filter"] = qdrant_filter
  1143	
  1144	            try:
  1145	                response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
  1146	                response.raise_for_status()
  1147	
  1148	                data = response.json().get("result", {})
  1149	                points = data.get("points", [])
  1150	
  1151	                # Return list of points with id and payload
  1152	                return [{"id": str(p["id"]), "payload": p.get("payload", {})} for p in points]
  1153	
  1154	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
  1155	                logger.error(f"Qdrant scroll failed: {e.response.status_code}")
  1156	                return []
  1157	            except Exception as e:
  1158	                logger.error("Qdrant scroll request error: %s", e)
  1159	                return []
  1160	
  1161	        except Exception as e:
  1162	            logger.error("Error scrolling Qdrant collection: %s", e)
  1163	            return []

  1165	    async def scroll_strict(
  1166	        self,
  1167	        *,
  1168	        metadata_filter: dict[str, Any],
  1169	        page_size: int = 256,
  1170	        max_points: int = 10_000,
  1171	    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1172	        """Return all matching points or raise; intended for safety invariants."""
  1173	        if not metadata_filter:
  1174	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter cannot be empty")
  1175	        if page_size <= 0 or max_points <= 0:
  1176	            raise ValueError("page_size and max_points must be positive")
  1177	
  1178	        qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1179	            metadata_filter,
  1180	            include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1181	        )
  1182	        if not qdrant_filter:
  1183	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter did not produce a Qdrant filter")
  1184	
  1185	        client = await self._get_client()
  1186	        url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/scroll"
  1187	        points: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1188	        offset: Any | None = None
  1189	
  1190	        while True:
  1191	            remaining = max_points - len(points)
  1192	            if remaining <= 0:
  1193	                raise RuntimeError("Qdrant strict scroll exceeded max_points")
  1194	            payload: dict[str, Any] = {
  1195	                "limit": min(page_size, remaining),
  1196	                "with_payload": True,
  1197	                "with_vectors": False,
  1198	                "filter": qdrant_filter,
  1199	            }
  1200	            if offset is not None:
  1201	                payload["offset"] = offset
  1202	
  1203	            response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
  1204	            response.raise_for_status()
  1205	            result = response.json().get("result", {})
  1206	            batch = result.get("points", [])
  1207	            points.extend(
  1208	                {"id": str(point["id"]), "payload": point.get("payload", {})}
  1209	                for point in batch
  1210	            )
  1211	            offset = result.get("next_page_offset")
  1212	            if offset is None:
  1213	                return points
  1214	
  1215	    async def peek(
  1216	        self,
  1217	        limit: int = 10,
  1218	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1219	        """
  1220	        Peek at points in the collection (Qdrant-compatible interface).
  1221	
  1222	        Args:
  1223	            limit: Maximum number of points to return
  1224	
  1225	        Returns:
  1226	            Dictionary with sample points in Qdrant format
  1227	        """
  1228	        try:
  1229	            client = await self._get_client()
  1230	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/scroll"
  1231	
  1232	            payload = {"limit": limit, "with_payload": True, "with_vectors": False}
  1233	            try:
  1234	                response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
  1235	                response.raise_for_status()
  1236	
  1237	                data = response.json().get("result", {})
  1238	                points = data.get("points", [])
  1239	
  1240	                # Transform to Qdrant format
  1241	                return {
  1242	                    "ids": [str(p["id"]) for p in points],
  1243	                    "documents": [p.get("payload", {}).get("text", "") for p in points],
  1244	                    "metadatas": [_extract_point_metadata(p.get("payload", {})) for p in points],
  1245	                }
  1246	
  1247	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
  1248	                logger.error(f"Qdrant peek failed: {e.response.status_code}")
  1249	                return {"ids": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
  1250	            except Exception as e:
  1251	                logger.error("Qdrant peek request error: %s", e)
  1252	                return {"ids": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
  1253	
  1254	        except Exception as e:
  1255	            logger.error("Error peeking Qdrant collection: %s", e)
  1256	            return {"ids": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}

  1258	    async def hybrid_search(
  1259	        self,
  1260	        query_embedding: list[float],
  1261	        query_sparse: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  1262	        filter: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  1263	        limit: int = 5,
  1264	        prefetch_limit: int = 20,
  1265	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1266	        """
  1267	        Hybrid search combining dense and sparse (BM25) vectors with RRF fusion.
  1268	
  1269	        Uses Qdrant's prefetch + fusion API for optimal hybrid search.
  1270	
  1271	        Args:
  1272	            query_embedding: Dense query embedding vector (1536 dims)
  1273	            query_sparse: Sparse query vector {"indices": [...], "values": [...]}
  1274	            filter: Metadata filter (simplified format)
  1275	            limit: Maximum number of final results
  1276	            prefetch_limit: Number of candidates to prefetch from each vector type
  1277	
  1278	        Returns:
  1279	            Dictionary with search results in standard format
  1280	        """
  1281	        # If no sparse vector provided, fall back to dense-only search
  1282	        if not query_sparse or not query_sparse.get("indices"):
  1283	            return await self.search(query_embedding, filter=filter, limit=limit)
  1284	
  1285	        async def _do_hybrid_search():
  1286	            client = await self._get_client()
  1287	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/query"
  1288	
  1289	            # Build prefetch queries for both dense and sparse
  1290	            prefetch = [
  1291	                {
  1292	                    "query": query_embedding,
  1293	                    "using": "dense",
  1294	                    "limit": prefetch_limit,
  1295	                },
  1296	                {
  1297	                    "query": {
  1298	                        "indices": query_sparse["indices"],
  1299	                        "values": query_sparse["values"],
  1300	                    },
  1301	                    "using": "bm25",
  1302	                    "limit": prefetch_limit,
  1303	                },
  1304	            ]
  1305	
  1306	            # Main query with RRF fusion
  1307	            payload = {
  1308	                "prefetch": prefetch,
  1309	                "query": {"fusion": "rrf"},  # Reciprocal Rank Fusion
  1310	                "limit": limit,
  1311	                "with_payload": True,
  1312	            }
  1313	
  1314	            # Add filter if provided
  1315	            if filter:
  1316	                qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1317	                    filter,
  1318	                    include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1319	                )
  1320	                if qdrant_filter:
  1321	                    payload["filter"] = qdrant_filter
  1322	
  1323	            try:
  1324	                response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
  1325	                response.raise_for_status()
  1326	
  1327	                data = response.json()
  1328	                results = data.get("result", {}).get("points", [])
  1329	
  1330	                # Transform results to standard format
  1331	                formatted_results = {
  1332	                    "ids": [str(r["id"]) for r in results],
  1333	                    "documents": [r["payload"].get("text", "") for r in results],
  1334	                    "metadatas": [_extract_point_metadata(r["payload"]) for r in results],
  1335	                    "distances": [1.0 - r.get("score", 0) for r in results],
  1336	                    "scores": [r.get("score", 0) for r in results],
  1337	                    "total_found": len(results),
  1338	                    "search_type": "hybrid_rrf",
  1339	                }
  1340	
  1341	                logger.debug(
  1342	                    f"Hybrid search: collection={self.collection_name}, "
  1343	                    f"found {len(results)} results (RRF fusion)",
  1344	                )
  1345	                return formatted_results
  1346	
  1347	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
  1348	                error_text = e.response.text if hasattr(e.response, "text") else str(e.response)
  1349	                error_lower = error_text.lower()
  1350	                # If hybrid search fails (e.g., collection doesn't have sparse/named vectors),
  1351	                # fall back to dense-only search
  1352	                # Covers: "sparse" errors, "dense" errors, "Vector params" errors
  1353	                if e.response.status_code == 400 and (
  1354	                    "sparse" in error_lower
  1355	                    or "dense" in error_lower
  1356	                    or "vector params" in error_lower
  1357	                ):
  1358	                    logger.warning(
  1359	                        f"Hybrid search not available for {self.collection_name}, "
  1360	                        "falling back to dense search",
  1361	                    )
  1362	                    return await self.search(query_embedding, filter=filter, limit=limit)
  1363	                logger.error(
  1364	                    f"Qdrant hybrid search failed: {e.response.status_code} - {error_text}",
  1365	                )
  1366	                return {
  1367	                    "ids": [],
  1368	                    "documents": [],
  1369	                    "metadatas": [],
  1370	                    "distances": [],
  1371	                    "scores": [],
  1372	                    "total_found": 0,
  1373	                    "search_type": "hybrid_rrf",
  1374	                    "error": error_text,
  1375	                }
  1376	            except Exception as e:
  1377	                logger.error("Qdrant hybrid search request error: %s", e)
  1378	                raise QdrantConnectionError(f"Qdrant connection error: {e}") from e
  1379	
  1380	        start_time = time.time()
  1381	        # 🔍 TRACING: Span for Qdrant hybrid search
  1382	        with trace_span(
  1383	            "qdrant.hybrid_search",
  1384	            {
  1385	                "collection": self.collection_name,
  1386	                "limit": limit,
  1387	                "prefetch_limit": prefetch_limit,
  1388	                "has_filter": bool(filter),
  1389	                "search_type": "hybrid_rrf",
  1390	            },
  1391	        ):
  1392	            try:
  1393	                result = await _retry_with_backoff(_do_hybrid_search)
  1394	                elapsed = time.time() - start_time
  1395	                _qdrant_metrics["search_calls"] += 1
  1396	                _qdrant_metrics["search_total_time"] += elapsed
  1397	                set_span_attribute("results_count", result.get("total_found", 0))
  1398	                set_span_attribute("latency_ms", int(elapsed * 1000))
  1399	                set_span_status("ok")
  1400	                logger.debug(f"Qdrant hybrid search completed in {elapsed * 1000:.2f}ms")

  1241	    async def hybrid_search_with_reranking(
  1242	        self,
  1243	        query: str,
  1244	        user_level: int,
  1245	        limit: int = 5,
  1246	        tier_filter: list[TierLevel] = None,
  1247	        collection_override: str | None = None,
  1248	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1249	        """
  1250	        Full hybrid search pipeline: BM25 + Dense + RRF + reranking.
  1251	
  1252	        This is the most comprehensive search method combining:
  1253	        1. Dense vector search (semantic)
  1254	        2. BM25 sparse vector search (keyword)
  1255	        3. Reciprocal Rank Fusion (combining both)
  1256	        4. CrossEncoder or Ze-Rank reranking (final precision)
  1257	
  1258	        Args:
  1259	            query: Search query
  1260	            user_level: User access level (0-3)
  1261	            limit: Max results (final count after all stages)
  1262	            tier_filter: Optional specific tier filter
  1263	            collection_override: Force specific collection
  1264	
  1265	        Returns:
  1266	            Search results with full pipeline metadata
  1267	        """
  1268	        pipeline_start_time = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1269	
  1270	        # 1. Hybrid search with overfetch for reranking
  1271	        initial_limit = limit * 3
  1272	        results = await self.hybrid_search(
  1273	            query=query,
  1274	            user_level=user_level,
  1275	            limit=initial_limit,
  1276	            tier_filter=tier_filter,
  1277	            collection_override=collection_override,
  1278	            apply_filters=True,
  1279	        )
  1280	
  1281	        # 2. Early exit for high-confidence results
  1282	        if results["results"] and results["results"][0].get("score", 0) > 0.85:
  1283	            logger.info(
  1284	                f"⚡ Early exit: Top result score {results['results'][0]['score']:.3f} > 0.85",
  1285	            )
  1286	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE:
  1287	                rag_early_exit_total.inc()
  1288	            results["results"] = results["results"][:limit]
  1289	            results["reranked"] = False
  1290	            results["early_exit"] = True
  1291	            return results
  1292	
  1293	        # 3. Re-rank with Ze-Rank 2
  1294	        reranker = self._init_reranker()
  1295	        if reranker.enabled:
  1296	            logger.info(
  1297	                f"🔍 Re-ranking {len(results['results'])} hybrid candidates for: '{query[:50]}'",
  1298	            )
  1299	            rerank_start = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1300	            reranked_docs = await reranker.rerank(query, results["results"], top_k=limit)

   338	def _formatter_backed_retriever() -> FakeRetriever:
   339	    """A retriever whose hits are minted by the REAL formatter.
   340	
   341	    Case 10 is a CROSSING test, so its inputs may not be hand-written dicts
   342	    carrying a hand-written `score_kind`: that would assert the test's own
   343	    imagination rather than the chain. These hits are produced by calling
   344	    `format_search_results` exactly as `SearchService.hybrid_search` calls it
   345	    on the hybrid branch — the same function, the same declared kind — so what
   346	    the package builder receives here has the shape production hands it.
   347	
   348	    The first draft of case 10 used the shared `_visa_retriever()` fake, whose
   349	    hits declare no kind at all. It passed on key-presence alone while every
   350	    source reached the scorer as UNKNOWN, which is the precise failure the
   351	    strengthened assertion below exists to catch.
   352	    """
   353	    raw = {
   354	        "ids": ["1", "2"],
   355	        "documents": [
   356	            "KITAS requires a sponsor letter and passport copy.",
   357	            "Immigration law UU 6/2011 governs stay permits.",
   358	        ],
   359	        "metadatas": [{}, {}],
   360	        # 1/(2+0) and 1/(2+1): first and second of ONE contributing list,
   361	        # from the measured server-side RRF table.
   362	        "distances": [1.0 - 0.5, 1.0 - (1.0 / 3.0)],
   363	        "scores": [0.5, 1.0 / 3.0],
   364	        "total_found": 2,
   365	    }
   366	    formatted = format_search_results(
   367	        raw,
   368	        "visa_oracle",
   369	        primary_collection=None,
   370	        query=VISA_QUERY,
   371	        score_kind=score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED,
   372	    )
   373	    return FakeRetriever({"visa_oracle": formatted})
   374

   376	# ============================================================================
   377	# Case 10 — PROJECTION test (staff-room requirement 2): the list handed to
   378	# calculate_evidence_score at wa_package_builder.py:594-598 carries
   379	# score_kind on EVERY element. Fails if the projection is ever re-narrowed
   380	# to {"score": ...} only.
   381	# ============================================================================
   382	
   383	
   384	@pytest.mark.asyncio
   385	async def test_10_scorer_projection_carries_score_kind_on_every_element(
   386	    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
   387	) -> None:
   388	    captured: dict[str, list] = {}
   389	
   390	    def fake_calculate_evidence_score(*, sources, context_gathered, query):
   391	        captured["sources"] = sources
   392	        return 0.5
   393	
   394	    monkeypatch.setattr(wpb_module, "calculate_evidence_score", fake_calculate_evidence_score)
   395	
   396	    await wpb_module.build_context_package(
   397	        query=VISA_QUERY,
   398	        history=[],
   399	        thread_epoch=0,
   400	        retriever=_formatter_backed_retriever(),
   401	    )
   402	
   403	    assert captured.get("sources"), "expected at least one source handed to the scorer"
   404	    for source in captured["sources"]:
   405	        assert score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY in source
   406	    # Presence alone is not enough: stamping every element UNKNOWN would also
   407	    # satisfy the loop above while destroying the contract's whole point. At
   408	    # least one element must carry a kind that a WRITER actually declared.
   409	    declared = {
   410	        source[score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY] for source in captured["sources"]
   411	    } - {score_provenance.UNKNOWN}
   412	    assert declared, (
   413	        "every source reached the scorer as UNKNOWN — the projection kept the key "
   414	        "but the chain lost the provenance somewhere upstream"
   415	    )

   444	# ============================================================================
   445	# Case 12 (addition, R2 backward-compat condition — staff-room ack
   446	# 2026-09-10T20:54:08Z, reiterated by the Dux): `package_hash` is
   447	# persisted and RE-VERIFIED ACROSS THE DEPLOY BOUNDARY
   448	# (services/integrations/wa_broker.py:468/691/885,
   449	# app/routers/wa_broker.py:279, app/routers/wa_package.py:161,
   450	# services/integrations/wa_codex_daemon.py:342) — so the question is not
   451	# "does the new hash differ" but "does a package sealed BEFORE this PR
   452	# still verify after it". `_canonical_wire`/`_package_hash` have no
   453	# per-field allowlist (spec R2): they are content-agnostic
   454	# `json.dumps(sort_keys=True)` over whatever they are given, so a stored
   455	# dict lacking score_kind/score_raw must re-hash to its own original value,
   456	# unchanged.
   457	# ============================================================================
   458	
   459	
   460	def test_a_package_sealed_before_score_kind_existed_still_verifies() -> None:
   461	    """A pre-PR sealed package — chunks with NO score_kind/score_raw key at
   462	    all — must still verify under the NEW code.
   463	
   464	    The proof is `ContextPackage.__post_init__` (wa_package_builder.py
   465	    :153-170) itself NOT raising on construction: it recomputes
   466	    `_canonical_wire` over the given fields and raises ValueError if the
   467	    digest doesn't match `package_hash`. That IS the real verification the
   468	    deploy boundary relies on when it reloads a stored package — this test
   469	    exercises that exact path rather than re-implementing the hash check.
   470	    """
   471	    history = [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}]
   472	    chunks = [{"collection": "visa_oracle", "text": "legacy chunk, no score_kind", "score": 0.7}]
   473	    assert score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY not in chunks[0]  # the pre-PR shape, made explicit
   474	    pricing_block = None
   475	    persona_digest = "pd"
   476	    evidence_inputs = {
   477	        "evidence_score": 0.5,
   478	        "context_length": 1,
   479	        "domain": "visa",
   480	        "label_threshold": 0.12,
   481	        "abstain": False,
   482	        "dlp": False,
   483	    }
   484	    thread_epoch = 1
   485	
   486	    # The digest a pre-PR build would have sealed — computed once, exactly
   487	    # as the original build did, never recomputed again below.
   488	    package_hash = wpb_module._package_hash(
   489	        history=history,
   490	        chunks=chunks,
   491	        pricing_block=pricing_block,
   492	        persona_digest=persona_digest,
   493	        evidence_inputs=evidence_inputs,
   494	        thread_epoch=thread_epoch,
   495	    )
   496	
   497	    # __post_init__ re-verifies digest == wire; constructing without a
   498	    # raised ValueError IS the proof that the pre-PR digest still holds.
   499	    package = ContextPackage(
   500	        history=history,
   501	        chunks=chunks,
   502	        pricing_block=pricing_block,
   503	        persona_digest=persona_digest,
   504	        evidence_inputs=evidence_inputs,
   505	        thread_epoch=thread_epoch,
   506	        package_hash=package_hash,
   507	    )
   508	    assert package.package_hash == package_hash
