    52	class LRUCache:
    53	    """
    54	    LRU Cache with TTL support for in-memory fallback.
    55	    Automatically evicts least recently used items when max size reached.
    56	    """
    57	
    58	    def __init__(
    59	        self,
    60	        max_size: int = DEFAULT_MAX_MEMORY_CACHE_SIZE,
    61	        maxsize: int | None = None,  # Alias for max_size (for compatibility)
    62	        default_ttl: int = DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL,
    63	    ) -> None:
    64	        """
    65	        Initialize LRU cache.
    66	
    67	        Args:
    68	            max_size: Maximum number of items to store
    69	            maxsize: Alias for max_size (for compatibility with tests)
    70	            default_ttl: Default TTL in seconds
    71	        """
    72	        self.max_size = maxsize if maxsize is not None else max_size
    73	        self.default_ttl = default_ttl
    74	        self.cache: OrderedDict[str, tuple[Any, float]] = (
    75	            OrderedDict()
    76	        )  # key -> (value, expire_time)
    77	
    78	    def get(self, key: str) -> Any | None:
    79	        """Get value from cache if not expired."""
    80	        if key not in self.cache:
    81	            return None
    82	
    83	        value, expire_time = self.cache[key]
    84	
    85	        # Check if expired
    86	        if time.time() > expire_time:
    87	            del self.cache[key]
    88	            return None
    89	
    90	        # Move to end (most recently used)
    91	        self.cache.move_to_end(key)
    92	        return value
    93	
    94	    def set(self, key: str, value: Any, ttl: int | None = None) -> None:
    95	        """Set value in cache with TTL."""
    96	        ttl = ttl if ttl is not None else self.default_ttl
    97	        expire_time = time.time() + ttl
    98	
    99	        if key in self.cache:
   100	            # Update existing
   101	            self.cache.move_to_end(key)
   102	        else:
   103	            # Check if we need to evict
   104	            if len(self.cache) >= self.max_size:
   105	                # Remove least recently used (first item)
   106	                self.cache.popitem(last=False)
   107	
   108	        self.cache[key] = (value, expire_time)
   109	
   110	    def delete(self, key: str) -> bool:
   111	        """Delete key from cache."""
   112	        if key in self.cache:
   113	            del self.cache[key]
   114	            return True
   115	        return False
   116	
   117	    def clear(self) -> int:
   118	        """Clear all cache entries."""
   119	        count = len(self.cache)
   120	        self.cache.clear()
   210	
   211	        filtered_kwargs = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if self._is_serializable(v)}
   212	
   213	        key_data = json.dumps(
   214	            {"args": filtered_args, "kwargs": filtered_kwargs},
   215	            sort_keys=True,
   216	            default=str,
   217	        )
   218	        key_hash = hashlib.md5(key_data.encode()).hexdigest()[:CACHE_KEY_HASH_LENGTH]
   219	        return f"zantara:{prefix}:{key_hash}"
   220	
   221	    async def get(self, key: str) -> Any | None:
   222	        """Get value from cache"""
   223	        self._try_connect_redis()
   224	        try:
   225	            if self.redis_available and self.redis_client:
   226	                value = await self.redis_client.get(key)
   227	                if value:
   228	                    self.stats["hits"] += 1
   229	                    return json.loads(value)
   230	                self.stats["misses"] += 1
   231	                return None
   232	            value = self._memory_cache.get(key)
   233	            if value is not None:
   234	                self.stats["hits"] += 1
   235	                return value
   236	            self.stats["misses"] += 1
   237	            return None
   238	        except Exception as e:
   239	            logger.error("Cache get error: %s", e)
   240	            self.stats["errors"] += 1
   241	            return None
   242	
   243	    async def set(self, key: str, value: Any, ttl: int | None = None) -> bool:
   244	        """Set value in cache with TTL (seconds). Uses RedisManager TTL strategy if ttl not provided."""
   245	        self._try_connect_redis()
   246	        if ttl is None:
   247	            # Extract prefix from key format "zantara:{prefix}:{hash}"
   248	            from backend.core.redis_manager import get_ttl
   249	
   250	            parts = key.split(":")
   251	            prefix = parts[1] if len(parts) >= 3 else "default"
   252	            ttl = get_ttl(prefix)
   253	        try:
   254	            if self.redis_available and self.redis_client:
   255	                await self.redis_client.setex(key, ttl, json.dumps(value, cls=DecimalEncoder))
   256	                return True
   257	            self._memory_cache.set(key, value, ttl)
   258	            return True
   259	        except Exception as e:
   260	            logger.error("Cache set error: %s", e)
   261	            self.stats["errors"] += 1
   262	            return False
   263	
   264	    async def delete(self, key: str) -> bool:
   265	        """Delete key from cache"""
    85	)

    87	# Fixed, documented access level for this deterministic, unauthenticated WA
    88	# path — matches `VectorSearchTool.__init__`'s own default (tools.py:89),
    89	# the closest existing analog for "no elevated tier, anonymous caller".
    90	_SEARCH_USER_LEVEL = 1

    92	# Persona digest: a MINIMAL, deterministic set of the existing SSOT prompt
    93	# sections from backend/prompts/zantara_core.py (the sole prompt SSOT per
    94	# apps/backend-rag/CLAUDE.md "Prompt Architecture") — chosen because they
    95	# most directly constrain HOW a WA reply gets written (language selection,
    96	# citation discipline, greeting handling). Deliberately excluded: the
    97	# creator/team personas, the master template, and the internal-monologue /
    98	# escalation / crash-protocol sections — those are orchestration-only
    99	# (deciding what tools to call, how to escalate) and irrelevant to a
   100	# downstream text generator that receives an already-built evidence
   101	# package and just has to write the reply.
   102	_PERSONA_SECTIONS: tuple[str, ...] = (LANGUAGE_PROTOCOL, CITATION_RULES, GREETING_RULES)

   104	
   105	class PackageUnbuildable(Exception):
   106	    """The deterministic pipeline cannot build a package for this query.
   107	
   108	    The caller (the wa_package router) treats this as a ROUTE decision, not
   109	    an error: the whole row goes to the Gemini leg, never an LLM-borrowed
   110	    fallback inside the codex path (spec §2.2).
   111	    """
   112	
   113	    def __init__(self, reason: str) -> None:
   114	        self.reason = reason
   115	        super().__init__(reason)
   116	
   117	
   118	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   119	class ContextPackage:
   120	    """The deterministic context package for the codex-route WA generation leg.
   121	
   122	    Allowlist schema (spec §2.2) — EXACTLY these 7 fields serialize via
   123	    `to_payload()`. Adding an 8th field to this dataclass does NOT
   124	    automatically leak it into the wire payload: `to_payload()` builds the
   125	    dict literally (never `dataclasses.asdict()` plus extras), so the
   126	    allowlist is enforced by construction, not by convention.
   127	    """
   128	
   129	    history: list[dict[str, str]]
   130	    chunks: list[dict[str, Any]]
   131	    pricing_block: dict[str, Any] | None
   132	    persona_digest: str
   133	    evidence_inputs: dict[str, Any]
   134	    thread_epoch: int
   135	    package_hash: str
   136	    # DLP reversal map (G-P3), placeholder -> original PII value. Populated
   137	    # ONLY when `build_context_package(dlp=True)` redacted this package's
   138	    # history/chunks; empty otherwise. Deliberately EXCLUDED from
   139	    # `_canonical_wire`/`__post_init__`'s hash domain, from `to_payload()`
   140	    # and from `wire_text()` — it is not part of the 7-key allowlist and
   141	    # must never enter the wire package, the package_hash inputs, or a log
   142	    # line (spec: "never leaves Fly"). `repr=False` so an accidental
   143	    # `logger.debug(package)`/`repr(package)` cannot print original PII.
   144	    reversal_map: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict, repr=False, compare=False)
   145	    # Wire snapshot, sealed at construction (Codex S2 re-verdict r6,
   146	    # finding 2): `frozen=True` freezes the field BINDINGS only — the
   147	    # nested lists/dicts stay mutable, so serializing them lazily would let
   148	    # `pkg.history[-1]["content"] = ...` between build and offer divorce
   149	    # the wire bytes from package_hash. Serializing ONCE here, and
   150	    # verifying the hash against the snapshot in __post_init__, makes the
   151	    # pair immutable together for the object's whole life.
   152	    _wire: str = field(init=False, repr=False, compare=False, default="")
   153	
   154	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   155	        wire = _canonical_wire(
   156	            {
   157	                "history": self.history,
   158	                "chunks": self.chunks,
   159	                "pricing_block": self.pricing_block,
   160	                "persona_digest": self.persona_digest,
   161	                "evidence_inputs": self.evidence_inputs,
   162	                "thread_epoch": self.thread_epoch,
   163	            }
   164	        )
   165	        digest = hashlib.sha256(wire.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   166	        if digest != self.package_hash:
   167	            raise ValueError(
   168	                "package_hash does not cover this package's wire bytes "
   169	                "(hash/wire integrity is sealed at construction)"
   170	            )
   171	        object.__setattr__(self, "_wire", wire)
   172	
   173	    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   174	        """Serialize to the exact 7-key allowlist. No extras, ever.
   175	
   176	        Returns a DEEP COPY (S2 cross-family review, finding 5):
   177	        `frozen=True` freezes the field BINDINGS, not the mutable
   178	        lists/dicts inside them — handing out the internal references
   179	        would let a caller mutate content after `package_hash` was
   180	        computed, silently divorcing the payload from its own hash.
   181	
   182	        NOT the broker wire format: this 7-key view INCLUDES package_hash,
   183	        so its serialization can never equal the bytes the hash covers —
   184	        offering `json.dumps(to_payload())` as broker_jobs.package makes
   185	        every broker-side hash verification fail by construction (Codex S2
   186	        re-verdict r5, finding 1). The envelope the broker receives and
   187	        verifies is ``wire_text()``; the hash travels beside it.
   188	        """
   189	        return copy.deepcopy(
   190	            {
   191	                "history": self.history,
   192	                "chunks": self.chunks,
   193	                "pricing_block": self.pricing_block,
   194	                "persona_digest": self.persona_digest,
   195	                "evidence_inputs": self.evidence_inputs,
   196	                "thread_epoch": self.thread_epoch,
   197	                "package_hash": self.package_hash,
   198	            }
   199	        )
   200	
   201	    def wire_text(self) -> str:
   202	        """The EXACT bytes offered to the broker as broker_jobs.package —
   203	        and, verbatim, the bytes ``package_hash`` covers.
   204	
   205	        One serializer (``_canonical_wire``) produces both this string and
   425	            truncated_count,
   426	            _MAX_CHUNK_CHARS,
   427	        )
   428	    return capped[:_MAX_CHUNKS]
   429	
   430	
   431	def _canonical_wire(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   432	    """The ONE serializer for the broker envelope — used by BOTH
   433	    ``ContextPackage.wire_text()`` and ``_package_hash``, so the bytes on
   434	    the wire and the bytes under the digest are the same function's output
   435	    by construction (Codex S2 re-verdict r5, finding 1). `sort_keys=True`
   436	    makes it independent of dict insertion order; compact `separators` plus
   437	    `ensure_ascii=False` keep it deterministic for identical content."""
   438	    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
   439	
   440	
   441	def _package_hash(
   442	    history: list[dict[str, str]],
   443	    chunks: list[dict[str, Any]],
   444	    pricing_block: dict[str, Any] | None,
   445	    persona_digest: str,
   446	    evidence_inputs: dict[str, Any],
   447	    thread_epoch: int,
   448	) -> str:
   449	    """Content-addressed sha256 hex digest over the 6 non-hash fields —
   450	    digested over exactly ``_canonical_wire`` of them, i.e. exactly the
   451	    string ``wire_text()`` later emits."""
   452	    payload = {
   453	        "history": history,
   454	        "chunks": chunks,
   455	        "pricing_block": pricing_block,
   456	        "persona_digest": persona_digest,
   457	        "evidence_inputs": evidence_inputs,
   458	        "thread_epoch": thread_epoch,
   459	    }
   460	    return hashlib.sha256(_canonical_wire(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   461	
   462	
   463	async def build_context_package(
   464	    *,
   465	    query: str,
   550	    retrieved.sort(key=lambda c: (-float(c["score"]), c["collection"], c["text"]))
   551	    chunks.extend(retrieved)
   552	
   553	    chunks = _cap_chunks(chunks)
   554	
   555	    pricing_block: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   556	    if has_pricing_intent(query):
   557	        pricing_block = _sanitize_pricing_block(get_pricing_service().search_service(query))
   558	
   559	    payload_history = _sanitize_history(history, query)
   560	
   561	    # G-P3 DLP gate — runs AFTER chunks are capped and history sanitized,
   562	    # BEFORE evidence_inputs/package_hash are computed: everything hashed
   563	    # and sealed below is the REDACTED content, never the original (spec
   564	    # rule: "package_hash computed over the REDACTED content"). Fail-closed
   565	    # by construction — ANY exception from the redact step (detector bug,
   566	    # or wa_dlp's own >MAX_PLACEHOLDERS overflow guard) becomes
   567	    # PackageUnbuildable so the row falls off to the Gemini leg instead of
   568	    # shipping a partially-redacted or unredacted package.
   569	    #
   570	    # M1 (Kimi adversarial review, verified): `pricing_block["search_query"]`
   571	    # is the customer's RAW query, verbatim (pricing_service.py sets it from
   572	    # the same `query` argument `_sanitize_pricing_block` keeps untouched) —
   573	    # without threading it through the SAME redaction state as history, a
   574	    # NIK in the query would be redacted in `history[-1]["content"]` but
   575	    # ship unredacted a few keys over in `pricing_block.search_query`, into
   576	    # both the wire and the hash. Passed into `redact_package_fields` so it
   577	    # shares dedup/placeholder-numbering with the history copy of the same
   578	    # string, then spliced back before hashing.
   579	    reversal_map: dict[str, str] = {}
   580	    if dlp:
   581	        pricing_search_query = (
   582	            pricing_block.get("search_query") if pricing_block is not None else None
   583	        )
   584	        try:
   585	            dlp_result = redact_package_fields(payload_history, chunks, pricing_search_query)
   586	        except Exception as exc:
   587	            logger.info(
   588	                "wa_package_builder: dlp redaction failed error=%s",
   589	                type(exc).__name__,
   590	            )
   591	            raise PackageUnbuildable(reason="dlp_error") from exc
   592	        payload_history = dlp_result.history
   593	        chunks = dlp_result.chunks
   594	        reversal_map = dlp_result.reversal_map
   595	        if pricing_block is not None:
   596	            pricing_block = {**pricing_block, "search_query": dlp_result.search_query}
   597	
   598	    persona_digest = _build_persona_digest()
   599	
   600	    # Evidence inputs are FROZEN here — finalization (spec §2.3) reads these
   601	    # fields, it never recomputes them against a possibly-drifted retrieval.
   602	    abstain_policy = build_abstain_policy(query)
   603	    evidence_score = calculate_evidence_score(
   604	        sources=[
   605	            {"score": chunk["score"], "score_kind": score_provenance.kind_of(chunk)}
   606	            for chunk in chunks
   607	        ],
   608	        context_gathered=[chunk["text"] for chunk in chunks],
   609	        query=query,
   610	    )
   611	    evidence_inputs: dict[str, Any] = {
   612	        "evidence_score": evidence_score,
   613	        "context_length": len(chunks),
   614	        "domain": plan.domain.value,
   615	        "label_threshold": abstain_policy.label_threshold,
   616	        "abstain": abstain_policy.label_abstains(evidence_score),
   617	        # Kimi-7 (MINOR): inside the hash domain by design — a future
   618	        # caller that forgets `dlp=True` produces a package distinguishable
   619	        # downstream (evidence_inputs is what finalize/consumers read out
   620	        # of the sealed wire) instead of one indistinguishable from a
   621	        # properly-redacted build.
   622	        "dlp": dlp,
   623	    }
   624	
   625	    package_hash = _package_hash(
   626	        history=payload_history,
   627	        chunks=chunks,
   628	        pricing_block=pricing_block,
   629	        persona_digest=persona_digest,
   630	        evidence_inputs=evidence_inputs,

   896	class CompleteStatus(str, enum.Enum):
   897	    ACCEPTED = "accepted"  # -> completed_pending_consume (or failed, typed)
   898	    REPLAY = "replay"  # same completion_key re-POST -> same 200
   899	    CONFLICT = "conflict"  # different payload for a completed job -> 409
   900	    GONE = "gone"  # no such leased job under this fence / expired -> 410
   260	    dependencies=[Depends(require_wa_broker_key)],
   261	)
   262	async def claim(
   263	    request: Request,
   264	    pool: asyncpg.Pool = Depends(get_database_pool),
   265	) -> ClaimResponse:
   266	    body = await _read_bounded_body(request, ClaimRequest)
   267	    async with pool.acquire() as conn:
   268	        row = await wa_broker.claim_job(
   269	            conn,
   270	            in_flight=body.in_flight,
   271	            last_exec_ms=body.last_exec_ms,
   272	        )
   273	    if row is None:
   274	        return ClaimResponse()
   275	    return ClaimResponse(
   276	        job_id=row["job_id"],
   277	        fence_token=row["fence_token"],
   278	        package=row["package"],
   279	        package_hash=row["package_hash"],
   280	        deadline_at=row["deadline_at"].isoformat(),
   281	        server_now=row["server_now"].isoformat(),
   282	    )

   209	class _Claim:
   210	    job_id: str

   218	class WaCodexDaemon:
   219	    """Single-flight claim → exec → complete loop against the WA broker."""
   220	
   221	    def __init__(
   222	        self,
   223	        config: DaemonConfig,
   224	        *,
   225	        codex_client: CodexExecClient | None = None,
   226	        http_client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None,
   227	        transport: httpx.AsyncBaseTransport | None = None,
   228	    ) -> None:
   229	        self._config = config
   230	        self._codex = codex_client or CodexExecClient(
   320	            )
   321	        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
   322	            logger.warning("wa-codex-daemon: claim transport error: %s", type(exc).__name__)
   323	            return None
   324	        if response.status_code != 200:
   325	            logger.warning("wa-codex-daemon: claim HTTP %s", response.status_code)
   326	            return None
   327	        try:
   328	            data = response.json()
   329	        except ValueError:
   330	            # A 200 whose body is not JSON (an LB error page, a truncated
   331	            # response) is a transport blip, not a loop crash — treat it
   332	            # like any failed claim (Kimi round-1 named test gap).
   333	            logger.warning("wa-codex-daemon: claim returned non-JSON 200")
   334	            return None
   335	        if not data.get("job_id"):
   336	            return None
   337	        try:
   338	            return _Claim(
   339	                job_id=str(data["job_id"]),
   340	                fence_token=str(data["fence_token"]),
   341	                package=str(data["package"]),
   342	                package_hash=str(data["package_hash"]),
   343	                deadline_at=str(data["deadline_at"]),
   344	                server_now=str(data["server_now"]),
   345	            )
   346	        except KeyError as exc:
   347	            # A job_id with missing siblings is a contract break — there is
   348	            # no fence to complete under, so nothing can be reported; the
   349	            # lease reaper folds it server-side at deadline.
   350	            logger.error("wa-codex-daemon: claim response missing field %s", exc)
   351	            return None
   352

   354	        self,
   355	        claim: _Claim,

   142	    async def execute(self, query: str, collection: str = None, top_k: int = 8, **kwargs) -> str:
   143	        """
   144	        Execute vector search.
   145	
   146	        If collection is specified: search only that collection.
   147	        If collection is None: federated search across ALL collections.
   148	        """
   149	        with trace_span(
   150	            "tool.vector_search",
   151	            {
   152	                "query_length": len(query),
   153	                "requested_collection": collection or "federated_all",
   154	                "top_k": top_k,
   155	            },
   156	        ):
   157	            top_k = int(top_k) if top_k else 8
   158

   173	                        doc = valid_docs[idx]
   174	                        doc["rerank_score"] = float(score)
   175	
   176	                        # Preserve original score if needed
   177	                        prior_score = doc.get("score")
   178	                        if "score" in doc and "vector_score" not in doc:
   179	                            doc["vector_score"] = doc["score"]
   180	
   181	                        # Update main score
   182	                        doc["score"] = float(score)
   183	                        score_provenance.stamp(
   184	                            doc,
   185	                            score_provenance.RERANKED,
   186	                            prior_score if isinstance(prior_score, (int, float)) else None,
   187	                        )
   188	                        reranked_docs.append(doc)
   189	
   190	                # Sort explicitly just in case API didn't
   191	                reranked_docs.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
   192	
   193	                set_span_attribute("results_count", len(reranked_docs))
   194	                if reranked_docs:
   195	                    set_span_attribute("top_score", round(reranked_docs[0]["score"], 4))
   196	                set_span_status("ok")
   197	
   198	                return reranked_docs[:top_k]
   199	
   200	            except Exception as e:
   201	                logger.error("❌ Re-ranking failed (Ze-Rank 2): %s", e)
   202	                set_span_status("error", str(e))
   203	                # Fallback to original order
   204	                return documents[:top_k]
   315	            doc_texts = []
   316	            valid_docs = []
   317	
   318	            for doc in documents:
   319	                text = doc.get("text") or doc.get("content") or ""
   320	                if text:
   321	                    doc_texts.append(text)
   322	                    valid_docs.append(doc)
   323	
   324	            if not doc_texts:
   325	                set_span_attribute("skipped", True)
   326	                set_span_attribute("skip_reason", "no_valid_text")
   327	                set_span_status("ok")
   328	                return documents[:top_k]
   329	
   330	            set_span_attribute("valid_documents_count", len(valid_docs))
   331	
   332	            try:
   333	                # Compute scores asynchronously
   334	                start_time = time.perf_counter()
   335	                scores = await self.compute_scores_async(query, doc_texts)
   336	                compute_time = (time.perf_counter() - start_time) * 1000
   337	                set_span_attribute("compute_time_ms", round(compute_time, 2))
   338	
   339	                # Attach scores to documents
   340	                reranked_docs = []
   341	                for doc, score in zip(valid_docs, scores, strict=False):
   342	                    # Create a copy to avoid mutating original
   343	                    doc_copy = doc.copy()
   344	
   345	                    # Store rerank score
   346	                    doc_copy["rerank_score"] = float(score)
   347	
   348	                    # Preserve original score
   349	                    prior_score = doc_copy.get("score")
   350	                    if "score" in doc_copy and "vector_score" not in doc_copy:
   351	                        doc_copy["vector_score"] = doc_copy["score"]
   352	
   353	                    # Update main score to rerank score
   354	                    doc_copy["score"] = float(score)
   355	                    score_provenance.stamp(
   356	                        doc_copy,
   357	                        score_provenance.RERANKED,
   358	                        prior_score if isinstance(prior_score, (int, float)) else None,
   359	                    )
   360	                    reranked_docs.append(doc_copy)
   361	
   362	                # Sort by rerank score (descending)
   363	                reranked_docs.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
   364	
   365	                set_span_attribute("results_count", len(reranked_docs))
   366	                if reranked_docs:
   367	                    set_span_attribute("top_score", round(reranked_docs[0]["score"], 4))
   368	                set_span_attribute("latency_ms", round(compute_time, 2))
   369	                set_span_status("ok")
   370	
   371	                logger.info(
   372	                    f"✅ Reranked {len(reranked_docs)} documents in {compute_time:.1f}ms "
   373	                    f"(model: {self.model_name})",
   374	                )

   302	def _default_kbli_dataset_path() -> Path:
   303	    """Resolve the canonical KBLI dataset in local and Fly layouts."""
   304	    module_path = Path(__file__).resolve()
   305	    for parent in module_path.parents:
   306	        for relative_path in (
   307	            Path("source_documents/KBLI_2025_FINAL_CLEAN.json"),
   308	            Path("data/source_documents/KBLI_2025_FINAL_CLEAN.json"),
   309	        ):
   310	            candidate = parent / relative_path
   311	            if candidate.is_file():
   312	                return candidate
   313	    # KBLIEye will log the missing-dataset failure and return an explicit
   314	    # DATABASE_NOT_LOADED state. Keep the expected Fly path in that log,
   315	    # without indexing beyond the shallower /app container hierarchy.
   316	    return Path("/app/source_documents/KBLI_2025_FINAL_CLEAN.json")

   319	class KBLICanonicalLookupTool(BaseTool):
   320	    """Exact lookup against the versioned KBLI 2025 canonical dataset.
   321	
   322	    Semantic search remains useful for discovering codes from a business
   323	    description. It is the wrong primitive once a five-digit code is already
   324	    present: a near neighbour can outrank the requested record. This tool is
   325	    deliberately exact and returns a compact subset of the canonical record
   355	
   356	
   357	def compute_evidence_score(
   358	    *,
   359	    trusted_tools_used: bool,
   360	    sources: list[Any] | None,
   361	    context_gathered: list[str] | None,
   362	    query: str,
   363	    log_prefix: str = "Evidence",
   364	) -> float:
   365	    """Return 0.85 if trusted tools succeeded, else keyword-based score."""
   366	    if trusted_tools_used:
   367	        # B1.1: this is a WHOLE-PACKAGE label (score_provenance.TRUSTED_TOOL_BYPASS),
   368	        # never a per-source score — `sources` is untouched on this branch.
   369	        # Named here only; the returned value stays exactly 0.85.
   370	        logger.info(
   371	            "🛡️ [%s] Trusted tools used: score=%.2f kind=%s",
   372	            log_prefix,
   373	            EVIDENCE_SCORE_TRUSTED_TOOL,
   374	            score_provenance.TRUSTED_TOOL_BYPASS,
   375	        )
   376	        return EVIDENCE_SCORE_TRUSTED_TOOL
   377	    # Lookup via the reasoning module so tests that patch
   378	    # `backend.services.rag.agentic.reasoning.calculate_evidence_score`
   379	    # continue to intercept the call.
   380	    from backend.services.rag.agentic import reasoning as _reasoning_module
   381	
   382	    evidence_score = _reasoning_module.calculate_evidence_score(
   383	        sources,
   384	        context_gathered,
   385	        query,
   386	    )
   387	    logger.info("🛡️ [%s] Keyword-based score: %.2f", log_prefix, evidence_score)
   388	    return evidence_score
   389	
   390	
   214	def calculate_evidence_score(
   215	    sources: list[dict] | None,
   216	    context_gathered: list[str],
   217	    query: str,
   218	) -> float:
   219	    """
   220	    Calculate evidence score based on source quality and context relevance.
   221	
   222	    FIXED: This score now properly reflects ACTUAL relevance between query and context.
   223	    - Mismatched results (e.g., KITAS query returning KBLI) will score < 0.15
   224	    - Nonsense queries ("xyzabc123") will score ~0.0
   225	    - Relevant results with good sources score 0.6+
   226	
   227	    Scoring Formula:
   228	        - Base score: 0.0
   229	        - Source quality component (0.0-0.4): Based on top source score
   230	        - Context relevance (0.0-0.6): Based on keyword/semantic overlap
   231	        - Maximum score: 1.0
   232	
   233	    Args:
   234	        sources: List of source dictionaries with 'score' field
   235	        context_gathered: List of context strings from tool results
   236	        query: Original user query string
   237	
   238	    Returns:
   239	        Evidence score between 0.0 and 1.0
   240	
   241	    Note:
   242	        - Score < 0.15: ABSTAIN (insufficient evidence)
   243	        - Score 0.15-0.6: CAUTIOUS (low confidence, use fallback)
   244	        - Score > 0.6: CONFIDENT (proceed with answer)
   245	    """
   246	    if not sources and not context_gathered:
   247	        return 0.0
   248	
   249	    # Extract meaningful query keywords (remove stop words, short words)
   250	    query_lower = query.lower()
   251	    stop_words = {
   252	        # English stop words
   253	        "the",
   254	        "a",
   255	        "an",
   256	        "is",
   257	        "are",
   258	        "was",
   259	        "were",
   260	        "be",
   590	    # Relevance is the PRIMARY factor - source quality only helps if relevance is good
   591	    # - Full match: keyword_match_ratio >= 0.5 → 0.6 points
   592	    # - Good match: keyword_match_ratio >= 0.3 → 0.4 points
   593	    # - Partial match: keyword_match_ratio >= 0.15 → 0.2 points
   594	    # - Poor match: keyword_match_ratio < 0.15 → 0.0 points (ABSTAIN territory)
   595	    if keyword_match_ratio >= 0.5:
   596	        semantic_relevance = 0.6  # Strong relevance
   597	    elif keyword_match_ratio >= 0.3:
   598	        semantic_relevance = 0.4  # Moderate relevance
   599	    elif keyword_match_ratio >= 0.15:
   600	        semantic_relevance = 0.2  # Weak relevance
   601	    else:
   602	        semantic_relevance = 0.0  # No relevant keywords found
   603	
   604	    # ========== SOURCE QUALITY SCORING ==========
   605	    # Source quality is SECONDARY - it can only boost score if relevance exists
   606	    source_quality_score = 0.0
   607	
   608	    if sources:
   609	        # Get top source score (best evidence)
   610	        source_scores = [s.get("score", 0.0) for s in sources if isinstance(s, dict)]
   611	
   612	        if source_scores:
   613	            top_score = max(source_scores)
   614	
   615	            # Source quality based on top score
   616	            # - Score 0.7+ : 0.4 points (excellent)
   617	            # - Score 0.5-0.7: 0.3 points (good)
   618	            # - Score 0.3-0.5: 0.2 points (acceptable)
   619	            # - Score < 0.3: 0.0-0.1 points (weak)
   620	            if top_score >= 0.7:
   621	                source_quality_score = 0.4
   622	            elif top_score >= 0.5:
   623	                source_quality_score = 0.3
   624	            elif top_score >= 0.3:
   625	                source_quality_score = 0.2
   626	            elif top_score >= 0.15:
   627	                source_quality_score = 0.1
   628	            else:
   629	                source_quality_score = 0.0
   630	
   631	    # ========== FINAL SCORE CALCULATION ==========
   632	    # Semantic relevance is the PRIMARY factor (60-80% weight)
   633	    # Source quality acts as a bonus/boost (20-40% weight)
   634	
   635	    if semantic_relevance == 0.0:
   636	        # No semantic relevance - score is based only on weak source quality
   637	        # This ensures nonsense queries or complete mismatches score < 0.15
   638	        final_score = min(source_quality_score * 0.2, 0.1)
   639	    elif semantic_relevance < 0.3:
   640	        # Weak relevance - cap the score regardless of source quality
   641	        # Max possible: 0.2 + (0.4 * 0.25) = 0.3
   642	        final_score = semantic_relevance + source_quality_score * 0.25
   643	        final_score = min(final_score, 0.35)
   644	    else:
   645	        # Good relevance - can add source quality as bonus
   646	        # But cap at 1.0 and weight source quality lower
   647	        final_score = semantic_relevance + source_quality_score * 0.5
   648	
   649	    # ========== SEMANTIC ALIGNMENT PENALTY (v3 quick-win #1) ==========
   650	    # Use Qdrant's per-source cosine similarity as a sanity gate. If retrieval
   651	    # returned sources but the BEST one has cosine < 0.5 with the query, the
   652	    # keyword-overlap path was probably misled (e.g. KITAS query → KBLI docs
   653	    # share the word "business" but are semantically unrelated). Penalize
   654	    # final score by 0.7× — does not create false negatives because a truly
   655	    # weak retrieval already had low source_quality_score; this just prevents
   656	    # the keyword-driven semantic_relevance from inflating the verdict.
   657	    #
   658	    # Source `score` is set by Qdrant (cosine similarity for our hybrid
   659	    # collections). Skip when sources are absent or top_score is missing.
   660	    if sources:
   661	        top_source_cosine = max(
   662	            (s.get("score", 0.0) for s in sources if isinstance(s, dict)),
   663	            default=0.0,
   664	        )
   665	        if 0 < top_source_cosine < 0.5 and final_score > 0.15:
   666	            penalized = round(final_score * 0.7, 2)
   667	            logger.info(
   668	                "evidence_score: semantic-alignment penalty applied (top_cosine=%.2f, %.2f → %.2f)",
   669	                top_source_cosine,
   670	                final_score,
   671	                penalized,
   672	            )
   673	            final_score = penalized
   674	
   675	    return round(min(final_score, 1.0), 2)
   676

   321	    async def search_with_reranking(
   322	        self,
   323	        query: str,
   324	        user_level: int,
   325	        limit: int = 5,
   326	        tier_filter: list[TierLevel] | None = None,
   327	        collection_override: str | None = None,
   328	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   329	        """
   330	        Enhanced search with Semantic Re-ranking.
   331	        Retrieves 3x candidates and re-ranks them for higher precision.
   332	        """
   333	        # 1. Retrieve more candidates (Wide Funnel)
   334	        initial_limit = limit * 3
   335	
   336	        results = await self.search(
   337	            query=query,
   338	            user_level=user_level,
   339	            limit=initial_limit,
   340	            tier_filter=tier_filter,
   341	            collection_override=collection_override,
   342	        )
   343	
   344	        # 2. Re-rank
   345	        self._init_reranker()
   346	        if self.reranker.enabled:
   347	            logger.info(f"🔍 Re-ranking {len(results['results'])} candidates for query: '{query}'")
   348	            reranked_docs = await self.reranker.rerank(query, results["results"], top_k=limit)
   349	            results["results"] = reranked_docs
   350	            results["reranked"] = True
   351	        else:
   352	            results["reranked"] = False
   353	            results["results"] = results["results"][:limit]
   354	
   355	        return results

   794	        per_query_limit = max(3, limit)
   795	        all_results: list[list[dict[str, Any]]] = []
   796	
   797	        async def _single_search(q: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   798	            try:
   799	                async with asyncio.timeout(8.0):
   800	                    res = await self._single_query_search(
   801	                        query=q,
   802	                        user_level=user_level,
   803	                        limit=per_query_limit,

   808	            except Exception as e:

   812	        # Structured concurrency (Python 3.11+); _single_search swallows exceptions.
   813	        async with asyncio.TaskGroup() as tg:
   814	            sub_tasks = [tg.create_task(_single_search(q)) for q in alt_queries]
   815	        sub_results = [t.result() for t in sub_tasks]
   816	        for sr in sub_results:
   817	            if sr:
   818	                all_results.append(sr)
   819	
   820	        if not all_results:
   821	            return {}
   822	
   823	        # RRF fusion across result sets
   824	        fused = self._rrf_fuse_multi(all_results, k=60)
   825	
   826	        # Deduplicate by document ID
   827	        seen_ids: set[str] = set()
   828	        deduped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   829	        for doc in fused:
   830	            doc_id = doc.get("id", "")
   831	            if doc_id and doc_id not in seen_ids:
   832	                seen_ids.add(doc_id)
   833	                deduped.append(doc)
   834	            elif not doc_id:
   835	                # No ID — dedup by first 100 chars of text
   836	                text_key = doc.get("text", "")[:100]
   837	                if text_key not in seen_ids:
   838	                    seen_ids.add(text_key)
   839	                    deduped.append(doc)
   840	
   841	        final_results = deduped[:limit]
   842

   844	            "query": query,
   845	            "results": final_results,
   846	            "user_level": user_level,
   847	            "allowed_tiers": [],

   932	
   933	        Checks ``settings.reranker_backend`` (env ``RERANKER_BACKEND``):
   934	          - ``"cross-encoder"`` -> local CrossEncoderReranker (free, ~75MB model)
   935	          - ``"zerank2"``       -> external Ze-Rank 2 API (requires API key)
   936	
   937	        When ``reranker_backend`` is ``"cross-encoder"``, the reranker is
   938	        explicitly enabled regardless of the legacy ``enable_reranker`` flag.
   939	        """
   940	        if not hasattr(self, "_reranker"):
   941	            backend: str = getattr(settings, "reranker_backend", "cross-encoder")
   942	            # HONESTY FIX (2026-07-18): this used to hardcode enabled=True
   943	            # regardless of settings.enable_reranker (default False — the
   944	            # cross-encoder model is intentionally NOT bundled in the prod
   945	            # image, saves ~5GB). That forced CrossEncoderReranker to try
   946	            # loading sentence_transformers on every call, fail with
   947	            # "No module named 'sentence_transformers'", and silently
   948	            # return zero scores — while callers still believed reranking
   949	            # ran. Respecting the setting here makes `.enabled` honest, so
   950	            # search_with_reranking()'s `if reranker.enabled:` check
   951	            # actually short-circuits instead of eating the failed-import
   952	            # cost on every query.
   953	            reranker_enabled = getattr(settings, "enable_reranker", False)
   954	            if backend == "cross-encoder":
   955	                from backend.services.rag.reranker import CrossEncoderReranker
   956	
   957	                self._reranker = CrossEncoderReranker(enabled=reranker_enabled)
   958	                logger.info(
   959	                    f"🔧 CrossEncoderReranker initialized: enabled={self._reranker.enabled}, "
   960	                    f"model={self._reranker.model_name}",
   961	                )
   962	            elif backend == "zerank2":
   963	                from backend.core.reranker import ReRanker
   964	
   965	                self._reranker = ReRanker()
   966	                logger.info(
   967	                    f"🔧 ReRanker (Ze-Rank 2) initialized: enabled={self._reranker.enabled}"
   968	                )
   969	            else:
   970	                logger.warning(
   971	                    "⚠️ Unknown reranker_backend '%s', falling back to cross-encoder",
   972	                    backend,
   973	                )
   974	                from backend.services.rag.reranker import CrossEncoderReranker
   975	
   976	                self._reranker = CrossEncoderReranker(enabled=reranker_enabled)
   977	        return self._reranker

    41	SCORE_KIND_KEY: Final = "score_kind"
    42	
    43	#: The key a source/chunk dict carries its pre-transform value under. Definition, and it
    44	#: is deliberately narrow: `score_raw` is the number THIS writer consumed to produce
    45	#: `score`. It is `None` when the value is synthetic (nothing was consumed) or when the
    46	#: producing step is not recoverable. It is never a second opinion about `score`.
    47	SCORE_RAW_KEY: Final = "score_raw"
    48	
    49	#: Server-side Reciprocal Rank Fusion (`core/qdrant_db.py:1308`) put through
    50	#: `result_formatter.py:89`. `score_raw` is the fusion output, in (0, 2].
    51	HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED: Final = "hybrid_rrf_formatted"
    52	
    53	#: A dense-only cosine put through the same formatter — either the explicit dense branch
    54	#: of `search_service` or the internal fallback at `core/qdrant_db.py:1362`. `score_raw`
    55	#: is the cosine.
    56	DENSE_FORMATTED: Final = "dense_formatted"
    57	
    58	#: A reranker overwrote `score` with its own relevance score (`core/reranker.py:169-180`,
    59	#: `services/rag/reranker.py:345-352`). `score_raw` is the value it replaced, which those
    60	#: writers also keep under `vector_score`.
    61	RERANKED: Final = "reranked"
    62	
    63	#: The curated-QA block minted at a literal 1.0 by `wa_package_builder.py:529`. Nothing was
    64	#: retrieved and nothing was scored, so `score_raw` is `None`.
    65	CURATED_SYNTHETIC: Final = "curated_synthetic"
    99	def kind_of(source: Any) -> str:
   100	    """Read a source's declared kind, defaulting to `UNKNOWN`.
   101	
   102	    Accepts anything, because the projections this travels through are typed `list[Any]`
   103	    and a non-dict source has, by definition, declared nothing.
   104	    """
   105	    if not isinstance(source, dict):
   106	        return UNKNOWN
   107	    kind = source.get(SCORE_KIND_KEY)
   108	    return kind if kind in SCORE_KINDS else UNKNOWN
   109	
   110	
   111	def raw_of(source: Any) -> float | None:
   112	    """Read a source's pre-transform value, or `None` when it has none or is unreadable."""
   113	    if not isinstance(source, dict):
   114	        return None
   115	    raw = source.get(SCORE_RAW_KEY)
   116	    return float(raw) if isinstance(raw, (int, float)) else None
   117	
   118	
   119	def stamp(
   120	    target: dict[str, Any],
   121	    kind: str,
   122	    raw: float | None = None,
   123	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   124	    """Declare a kind on a dict IN PLACE, additively, and return it.
   125	
   126	    Raises on an unknown kind: a writer that invents a spelling is a writer whose value the
   127	    consumers cannot read, and failing at the mint is cheaper than an `UNKNOWN` appearing
   128	    three layers downstream with nothing to trace it to.
   129	    """
   130	    if kind not in SCORE_KINDS:
   131	        raise ValueError(
   132	            f"unknown score_kind {kind!r}; the vocabulary is {sorted(SCORE_KINDS)}",
   133	        )
   134	    target[SCORE_KIND_KEY] = kind
   135	    if raw is not None:
   136	        target[SCORE_RAW_KEY] = float(raw)
   137	    return target

Riferimenti relativi a `apps/backend-rag/backend/`. Revisione del HEAD `4675f04bca`; nessuna modifica.

1. **NOT-A-DEFECT — R1 distingue correttamente i ritorni Qdrant.**  
   `core/qdrant_db.py:518,596,608,647,1282,1331,1362,1366,1408`; `services/search/search_service.py:1163`.  
   Tutti i ritorni dense omettono `search_type`; successo ed errore vuoto hybrid lo includono. I tre fallback hybrid delegano a `search()` senza aggiungerlo: non ho trovato controesempi in nessuna direzione.

2. **NOT-A-DEFECT — I nuovi metadati non modificano il calcolo numerico.**  
   `services/rag/agentic/reasoning_utils.py:214,610,662`; `_reasoning_evidence.py:366`.  
   Lo scorer accetta dizionari e legge esclusivamente `score`; aggiungere chiavi non cambia soglie o risultato. I lettori di `score_kind`/`score_raw` propagano metadati; il bypass conserva `0.85`. Le modifiche ai byte sigillati restano quelle autorizzate da R2.

3. **NOT-A-DEFECT — Nessun chiamante attuale passa un kind fuori vocabolario.**  
   `core/score_provenance.py:99,130`; `services/misc/result_formatter.py:145`; `core/reranker.py:183`; `services/rag/reranker.py:355`; `services/search/search_service.py:1112`; `services/rag/agentic/wa_package_builder.py:293,537`.  
   I chiamanti usano costanti ammesse, il default `UNKNOWN`, oppure `kind_of()`. Non ho trovato un percorso runtime che raggiunga il `ValueError` di `stamp()` con un kind sconosciuto.

4. **NOT-A-DEFECT — `0.0` viene conservato.**  
   `services/misc/result_formatter.py:134`; `core/score_provenance.py:115,135`; `core/reranker.py:186`; `services/rag/reranker.py:358`.  
   I controlli usano tipo numerico e `is not None`, non verità/falsità. Una sonda sul codice reale ha confermato `score_raw=0.0`.

5. **NOT-A-DEFECT — L’alias nella cache esiste, ma UNKNOWN non diventa provenienza nota.**  
   `core/cache.py:83,92,108,229,232`; `services/search/search_service.py:1107`.  
   Redis restituisce un oggetto deserializzato nuovo; il fallback LRU restituisce lo stesso riferimento, quindi un lettore successivo vede effettivamente `unknown` senza un ulteriore `cache.set()`: riprodotto. Tuttavia `UNKNOWN` significa esplicitamente “nessuna dichiarazione del produttore”, quindi questa mutazione non inventa un’origine né cambia un punteggio.

6. **NOT-A-DEFECT — R2 non introduce il confronto incompatibile ipotizzato.**  
   `services/integrations/wa_broker.py:679,885`; `app/routers/wa_broker.py:278`; `app/routers/wa_package.py:160`; `services/integrations/wa_codex_daemon.py:341,495`.  
   Il broker conserva e restituisce il pacchetto come TEXT; il daemon inoltra quella stringa, senza ricostruirla con il nuovo builder. Nei consumer indicati non esiste il confronto fra hash vecchio e pacchetto rigenerato nuovo; una sonda con hash prodotto dal codice base e `ContextPackage` nuovo ha inoltre verificato la compatibilità.

7. **NOT-A-DEFECT — Il doppio reranking esiste, ma `score_raw` conserva il valore appena sostituito.**  
   `services/search/search_service.py:1272,1300,1303`; `services/rag/reranker.py:349`; `core/score_provenance.py:43`.  
   Con cache in memoria, due chiamate possono rerankare lo stesso risultato: riprodotto `score=0.8`, `score_raw=0.7`, `vector_score=0.6`. È conforme alla definizione di `score_raw` come input dell’ultimo passaggio; è invece troppo generale il commento a `core/score_provenance.py:59` che lo equipara a `vector_score`, da circoscrivere al primo reranking.

8. **MEDIUM — CONFIRMED-DEFECT: il test di retrocompatibilità non congela l’oracolo vecchio.**  
   `tests/unit/services/rag/agentic/test_score_contract_crossing.py:460,488,499`.  
   `test_a_package_sealed_before_score_kind_existed_still_verifies` calcola il presunto hash vecchio con `_package_hash` nuovo e lo verifica con il serializer nuovo: una modifica coerente di entrambi può rompere i vecchi pacchetti lasciandolo verde. Ho eseguito esattamente quel test con l’implementazione base: passa anche senza la feature. **Fix:** usare wire e digest congelati dal base, verificando anche il rifiuto di byte alterati.  
   I valori numerici dei casi 1–4 sono corretti: `0.6667`, `1.0`, `0.5455` e `0.5/0.6667/0.9091`. La generalizzazione del floor `1/31` richiede però `prefetch_limit≤30`: `search_service.py:1271,1150` può superarlo tramite overfetch.

9. **BLOCKER — CONFIRMED-DEFECT: i writer ordinari continuano a produrre UNKNOWN.**  
   `services/misc/result_formatter.py:19`; `services/search/search_service.py:705,886`; `services/rag/agentic/tools.py:197,289`.  
   Solo il chiamante a `search_service.py:1184` dichiara il kind: `search()` e `_single_query_search()` lasciano il default `UNKNOWN` anche su risultati nuovi di origine nota. Con reranker disabilitato o early exit, questi risultati attraversano `VectorSearchTool`, `_reasoning_loop_helpers.py:302` e `reasoning.py:1375` fino allo scorer: la promessa “ogni writer dichiara l’origine” è falsa. **Fix:** cablare tutti i chiamanti pertinenti del formatter e aggiungere test sui percorsi ordinari, incluso reranking saltato.

10. **MEDIUM — CONFIRMED-DEFECT: la proiezione WA perde `score_raw` prima dello scorer.**  
    `services/rag/agentic/wa_package_builder.py:603`; `tests/unit/services/rag/agentic/test_score_contract_crossing.py:403`.  
    I chunk conservano il raw, ma la lista passata a `calculate_evidence_score` contiene soltanto `score` e `score_kind`. Il test verifica esclusivamente il kind, quindi non rileva questa interruzione della provenienza. **Fix:** propagare anche `score_raw` e verificarne il valore esatto all’ingresso dello scorer.

VERDICT: REWORK.

+"""The score contract: what the number a source carries IS, and where it came from.
+
+B1.1. Every `score` that can reach `calculate_evidence_score` is produced by one of a
+small number of writers, and until this module existed none of them said which. The
+scorer's own comment (`services/rag/agentic/reasoning_utils.py:650`) calls the field
+"Qdrant's per-source cosine similarity"; on the hybrid path it is a Reciprocal Rank
+Fusion output put through `1/(1+distance)`, which is a ranking transform and not a
+cosine. This module names the difference so a consumer can read it instead of guessing.
+
+MEASURED, not assumed (prod Qdrant 1.16.3, read-only probe, base sha 1d726045c9 —
+`evidence/2026-09/agent-nuzantara-backend-rag-b1-score-contract-107e45b6/measured-fusion-table.md`):
+server-side RRF returns `sum over contributing lists of 1/(2 + rank0)`, zero-based, ranking
+constant 2 — exact on all ten measured rows, with the k=60/1-based alternative refuted on
+all ten. With the production `prefetch_limit = limit * 3`, that is bounded below by 1/31,
+so `result_formatter.format_search_results` maps every hybrid result into [0.508, 1.000]
+before any collection boost, and the dense fallback into [0.5, 1.000] for any non-negative
+cosine. Consequences a reader needs before touching a threshold:
+
+  * a `hybrid_rrf_formatted` value near 0.5 means "last of a long list", NOT "half similar";
+  * `reasoning_utils.py:665`'s `0 < top < 0.5` guard cannot be entered from any live path;
+  * two of these kinds carry no retrieval behind them at all (`curated_synthetic`,
+    `trusted_tool_bypass`), so comparing them numerically to a retrieved value is
+    comparing a label with a measurement.
+
+TWO RULES, and they are the point of the module:
+
+1.  A kind is DECLARED by the writer that mints the value. It is NEVER inferred from the
+    number's magnitude — magnitudes overlap across kinds by construction (a rank-1-in-both
+    fusion hit and a curated block both arrive at 1.0), so any inference is a coin flip
+    wearing a heuristic's clothes.
+2.  A value whose writer did not declare a kind is `UNKNOWN`, and `UNKNOWN` is a fact about
+    our knowledge, not a low grade. A legacy cache entry written before this module existed
+    is `UNKNOWN`; so is anything reconstructed from a projection that dropped the key.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from typing import Any, Final
+
+#: The key a source/chunk dict carries its declared kind under.
+SCORE_KIND_KEY: Final = "score_kind"
+
+#: The key a source/chunk dict carries its pre-transform value under. Definition, and it
+#: is deliberately narrow: `score_raw` is the number THIS writer consumed to produce
+#: `score`. It is `None` when the value is synthetic (nothing was consumed) or when the
+#: producing step is not recoverable. It is never a second opinion about `score`.
+SCORE_RAW_KEY: Final = "score_raw"
+
+#: Server-side Reciprocal Rank Fusion (`core/qdrant_db.py:1308`) put through
+#: `result_formatter.py:89`. `score_raw` is the fusion output, in (0, 2].
+HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED: Final = "hybrid_rrf_formatted"
+
+#: A dense-only cosine put through the same formatter — either the explicit dense branch
+#: of `search_service` or the internal fallback at `core/qdrant_db.py:1362`. `score_raw`
+#: is the cosine.
+DENSE_FORMATTED: Final = "dense_formatted"
+
+#: A reranker overwrote `score` with its own relevance score (`core/reranker.py:169-180`,
+#: `services/rag/reranker.py:345-352`). `score_raw` is the value that reranking pass
+#: replaced — on a FIRST rerank that is the retrieval score those writers also keep under
+#: `vector_score`, but a result reranked twice carries the previous rerank's output, so
+#: `score_raw` and `vector_score` are not interchangeable in general.
+RERANKED: Final = "reranked"
+
+#: The curated-QA block minted at a literal 1.0 by `wa_package_builder.py:529`. Nothing was
+#: retrieved and nothing was scored, so `score_raw` is `None`.
+CURATED_SYNTHETIC: Final = "curated_synthetic"
+
+#: A knowledge-graph entity source from the KG fast path. Not a similarity.
+KG_ENTITY: Final = "kg_entity"
+
+#: The flat `EVIDENCE_SCORE_TRUSTED_TOOL` (`_reasoning_evidence.py:40`). This is a
+#: whole-package label rather than a per-source score, and it bypasses the ordinary
+#: scorer entirely; it is named here so the bypass is legible in the same vocabulary.
+TRUSTED_TOOL_BYPASS: Final = "trusted_tool_bypass"
+
+#: No writer declared a kind. Legacy cache entries and values that survived a projection
+#: which dropped the key land here. Never a synonym for "bad".
+UNKNOWN: Final = "unknown"
+
+SCORE_KINDS: Final[frozenset[str]] = frozenset(
+    {
+        HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED,
+        DENSE_FORMATTED,
+        RERANKED,
+        CURATED_SYNTHETIC,
+        KG_ENTITY,
+        TRUSTED_TOOL_BYPASS,
+        UNKNOWN,
+    },
+)
+
+#: The kinds that have real retrieval behind them, so a numeric comparison between two of
+#: them means something. `CURATED_SYNTHETIC` and `TRUSTED_TOOL_BYPASS` are deliberately
+#: absent: they are labels, not measurements.
+RETRIEVED_KINDS: Final[frozenset[str]] = frozenset(
+    {HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED, DENSE_FORMATTED, RERANKED},
+)
+
+
+def kind_of(source: Any) -> str:
+    """Read a source's declared kind, defaulting to `UNKNOWN`.
+
+    Accepts anything, because the projections this travels through are typed `list[Any]`
+    and a non-dict source has, by definition, declared nothing.
+    """
+    if not isinstance(source, dict):
+        return UNKNOWN
+    kind = source.get(SCORE_KIND_KEY)
+    return kind if kind in SCORE_KINDS else UNKNOWN
+
+
+def raw_of(source: Any) -> float | None:
+    """Read a source's pre-transform value, or `None` when it has none or is unreadable."""
+    if not isinstance(source, dict):
+        return None
+    raw = source.get(SCORE_RAW_KEY)
+    return float(raw) if isinstance(raw, (int, float)) else None
+
+
+def stamp(
+    target: dict[str, Any],
+    kind: str,
+    raw: float | None = None,
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """Declare a kind on a dict IN PLACE, additively, and return it.
+
+    Raises on an unknown kind: a writer that invents a spelling is a writer whose value the
+    consumers cannot read, and failing at the mint is cheaper than an `UNKNOWN` appearing
+    three layers downstream with nothing to trace it to.
+    """
+    if kind not in SCORE_KINDS:
+        raise ValueError(
+            f"unknown score_kind {kind!r}; the vocabulary is {sorted(SCORE_KINDS)}",
+        )
+    target[SCORE_KIND_KEY] = kind
+    if raw is not None:
+        target[SCORE_RAW_KEY] = float(raw)
+    return target

# B1.1 build spec — the score contract, pinned end to end

Base sha `1d726045c9`. Root for relative paths: `apps/backend-rag/backend/`.
Every file:line below was re-read on this base; the vocabulary module
`core/score_provenance.py` is ALREADY WRITTEN and is the contract's single home.

## The two design rulings the implementer must not relitigate

**R1 — provenance is DECLARED by the caller that knows, never derived from `search_type`.**
`core/qdrant_db.py` emits `"search_type": "hybrid_rrf"` on the hybrid path (`:1338`, `:1373`,
`:1389`) and emits NO `search_type` at all from the dense `search()` returns (`:519`, `:597`,
`:608`), so `services/search/search_service.py:1142`'s
`raw_results.get("search_type", "hybrid_rrf")` labels a dense fallback as hybrid. That is a real
defect and B1 does NOT fix it: `search_type` is a served field (it is the label of the
`rag_vector_search` metric family, `app/metrics.py:852`, and it is returned in the response and
written to the cache), so changing its value is a served-behaviour change and belongs to B2. It is
recorded as an inventory row and as a declared residual. Instead, `search_service` — which knows
from its own branch which call it made — PASSES the kind down. A wrong `search_type` therefore can
no longer make a wrong `score_kind`.

**R2 — provenance lives INSIDE the sealed bytes, and the digest changes.**
`_package_hash` (`services/rag/agentic/wa_package_builder.py:434-453`) hashes `chunks` as raw
dicts with no per-field allowlist, and `_canonical_wire` (`:424-431`) is `json.dumps(sort_keys=True)`
over whatever it is given, so a per-chunk `score_kind` enters both `package_hash` and `wire_text()`
automatically. That is the objective, not a side effect: B1's §1 goal is that "the sealed package
proves the scorer receives the same provenance whose bytes were sealed". `__post_init__`
(`:153-170`) re-verifies hash==wire, so the two stay self-consistent by construction.
**The digest therefore CHANGES, and the PR says so in plain words.** The STOP condition attached to
it (`B1-design.md` §4(d), "a digest change with rows in flight is a STOP") is measured and NOT
triggered: `wa_outbox` holds only terminal rows, 241 `failed` + 179 `done`, newest
2026-09-01 09:27:53Z, zero `pending`/`generating` — receipt in `measured-fusion-table.md`.
**`evidence_inputs` is NOT touched**, deliberately: `tests/unit/services/test_wa_codex_leg.py:159`
and `:1302` pin its exact JSON substring in the wire, and B2 can read the kinds off the chunks.
`context_length` is `len(chunks)` at `:601` (the file's only occurrence) and is key-agnostic, so it
does not move.

## The writers (additive only — no field renamed or removed, no numeric value changed)

| #   | File:line                                                                                                              | Value today                                         | Declared kind                                            | `score_raw`                                                                          |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 1   | `services/misc/result_formatter.py:89` via `search_service.py:1158` (hybrid branch)                                    | `1/(1+distance)` over server RRF                    | `hybrid_rrf_formatted`                                   | the fusion output, i.e. `raw_results["scores"][i]`                                   |
| 2   | same formatter via the dense branch (`search_service.py:1143-1154`) and the internal fallback `core/qdrant_db.py:1362` | `1/(1+distance)` over a cosine                      | `dense_formatted`                                        | the cosine, i.e. `raw_results["scores"][i]`                                          |
| 3   | `core/reranker.py:169-180` and `services/rag/reranker.py:345-352`                                                      | reranker `relevance_score` overwrites `score`       | `reranked`                                               | the value it replaced (the same number those writers already keep as `vector_score`) |

## Crossing tests (new file `tests/unit/services/rag/agentic/test_score_contract_crossing.py`)

Each asserts the EXACT numeric `score` and the `score_kind` that arrive at the scorer, crossing
producer → formatter/reranker → source projection → package construction → DLP/capping →
canonical serialization → scorer input:

1. rank-1 hybrid, in ONE list → fusion `0.5` → formatted `0.6667`, kind `hybrid_rrf_formatted`.
2. rank-1 hybrid, in BOTH lists → fusion `1.0` → formatted `1.0`, same kind.
3. rank-5 hybrid (`rank0=4`) in one list → fusion `0.1667` → formatted `0.5455`, same kind.
   (All three numbers are taken from the MEASURED table, not from a local re-derivation.)
4. dense fallback at cosine `0.0` / `0.5` / `0.9` → formatted `0.5` / `0.6667` / `0.9091`,
   kind `dense_formatted`.
5. a reranked source → kind `reranked`, `score_raw` == the pre-rerank value == `vector_score`.
6. curated synthetic `1.0` → kind `curated_synthetic`, `score_raw` absent.
7. one collection boost applied → the boosted number is unchanged from today AND the kind survives
   the boost (a boost does not change what the number IS).
8. an `unknown` cache hit → a cached entry with no `score_kind` reads back as `unknown`.
9. **NEGATIVE, must fail on origin/main:** a dense fallback whose `raw_results` carries no
   `search_type` is labelled `dense_formatted` even though `search_service.py:1142` still calls it
   `"hybrid_rrf"` — the two disagree ON PURPOSE and the test pins the disagreement, because the
   kind comes from the caller and the `search_type` lie is B2's to fix.
10. **PROJECTION test (staff-room requirement 2):** the list handed to
    `calculate_evidence_score` at `wa_package_builder.py:594-598` carries `score_kind` on every
    element. This is the test that fails if anyone re-narrows the projection to `{"score": ...}`.
11. A printed real package from the fixture path shows every source with its kind (PR body).

## Innocence

The six files at their origin/main counts or better: `test_evidence_cross_language.py`,
`test_evidence_scoring_abstain.py`, `test_wa_package_builder.py`, `test_wa_greeting.py`,
`test_curated_qa_government_fee_gate.py`, `test_wa_finalize.py`. Plus the widest scorer suites,
which this PR must not move: `tests/services/rag/agentic/test_reasoning_utils.py` (84),
`tests/unit/services/rag/agentic/test_reasoning.py` (37),
`tests/services/rag/test_confidence_scoring.py` (21). Any test that pins the WIRE or the
`package_hash` by literal value is expected to move, and every such move is listed in the PR body
with the reason "digest change, declared, no rows in flight".

   452	    async def _prepare_search_context(

   460	        """Prepare common context for search operations (DRY helper).
   461	
   462	        Extracts shared logic from search() and search_with_reranking():
   463	        - Generate query embedding
   464	        - Route to collection
   465	        - Get vector DB client
   466	        - Build tier/repealed filters
   467	        - Determine allowed tier values
   468	
   469	        Args:
   470	            query: Search query
   471	            user_level: User access level (0-3)
   472	            tier_filter: Optional tier restriction

   482	        # Validate input
   483	        if not query or not query.strip():
   484	            raise ValueError("Query cannot be empty")
   485	
   486	        if user_level < 0 or user_level > 3:
   487	            raise ValueError(f"User level must be between 0 and 3, got {user_level}")
   488	
   489	        # Expand query with keyword translations (IT/EN → EN/ID)
   490	        # This improves embedding match with Indonesian-language documents
   491	        from backend.services.search.keyword_translator import get_keyword_translator
   492	
   493	        translator = get_keyword_translator()
   494	        expanded_query = translator.translate(query)
   495	        if expanded_query != query:
   496	            logger.debug(f"Query expanded for embedding: '{query}' → '{expanded_query[:100]}...'")
   497	
   498	        # Generate query embedding — check process-level LRU cache first
   499	        # This avoids redundant OpenAI calls for: (a) repeated queries, (b) federated search
   500	        # where the same query is sent to 8 collections in parallel.
   501	        cache_key = expanded_query.strip().lower()
   502	        if cache_key in self._embedding_cache:
   503	            query_embedding = self._embedding_cache[cache_key]
   504	            self._embedding_cache.move_to_end(cache_key)
   505	            logger.debug("⚡ Embedding cache HIT — skipped OpenAI call")
   506	        else:
   507	            query_embedding = await self.embedder.generate_query_embedding(expanded_query)
   508	            # LRU eviction
   509	            if len(self._embedding_cache) >= self._embedding_cache_max:
   510	                self._embedding_cache.popitem(last=False)
   511	            self._embedding_cache[cache_key] = query_embedding
   512	
   513	        # Validate embedding was generated
   514	        if not query_embedding or len(query_embedding) == 0:
   515	            raise ValueError("Failed to generate query embedding")
   516	
   517	        # Route to appropriate collection
   518	        routing_info = await self._route_search_query(
   519	            query=query,
   520	            collection_override=collection_override,
   521	            enable_fallbacks=False,
   522	        )
   523	        collection_name = routing_info["collection_name"]
   524	
   525	        # Get vector DB client
   526	        vector_db = self.collection_manager.get_collection(collection_name)
   527	        if not vector_db:
   528	            logger.error("❌ Unknown collection: %s, defaulting to legal_unified", collection_name)
   529	            vector_db = self.collection_manager.get_collection("legal_unified")
   530	            collection_name = "legal_unified"
   531	            if not vector_db:
   532	                raise ValueError("Failed to initialize default collection")
   533	
   534	        # Determine allowed tiers
   535	        allowed_tiers = self.LEVEL_TO_TIERS.get(user_level, [])
   536	        if tier_filter:
   537	            allowed_tiers = [t for t in allowed_tiers if t in tier_filter]
   538	
   539	        # Build filter (only for zantara_books)
   540	        tier_filter_dict = None
   541	        if collection_name == "zantara_books" and allowed_tiers:
   542	            tier_values = [t.value for t in allowed_tiers]
   543	            tier_filter_dict = {"tier": {"$in": tier_values}}
   544	        else:
   545	            tier_values = []
   546	
   547	        # Build combined filter
   548	        chroma_filter = build_search_filter(
   549	            tier_filter=tier_filter_dict,
   550	            exclude_repealed=True,
   551	            exclude_historical=self._requires_current_law_guard(collection_name),
   552	        )
   553	
   554	        # Control filter application
   555	        if apply_filters is False:
   556	            # Tier/repeal tuning can be intentionally disabled by internal
   557	            # callers, but historical evidence is never eligible as current law.
   558	            chroma_filter = (
   559	                build_search_filter(
   560	                    exclude_repealed=False,
   561	                    exclude_historical=True,
   562	                )
   563	                if self._requires_current_law_guard(collection_name)
   564	                else None
   565	            )
   566	
   567	        return query_embedding, collection_name, vector_db, chroma_filter, tier_values

   569	    async def search(
   570	        self,
   571	        query: str,
   572	        user_level: int,
   573	        limit: int = 5,
   574	        tier_filter: list[TierLevel] = None,
   575	        collection_override: str | None = None,
   576	        apply_filters: bool | None = None,
   577	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   578	        """
   579	        Semantic search with tier-based access control and intelligent collection routing.
   580	
   581	        Args:
   582	            query: Search query
   583	            user_level: User access level (0-3)
   584	            limit: Max results
   585	            tier_filter: Optional specific tier filter
   586	            collection_override: Force specific collection (for testing)
   587	            apply_filters: If True, apply tier/exclude_repealed filters. If None, uses default
   588	                          behavior (filters disabled for backward compatibility with chat path).
   589	
   590	        Returns:
   591	            Search results with metadata
   592	        """
   593	        try:
   594	            # Multi-query expansion: generate 2-3 alternative queries, search each, RRF fusion
   595	            if (
   596	                getattr(settings, "enable_query_expansion", False)
   597	                and len(query.split()) > 4
   598	                and not collection_override  # skip for targeted single-collection searches
   599	            ):
   600	                try:
   601	                    multi_result = await self._search_with_multi_expansion(
   602	                        query=query,
   603	                        user_level=user_level,
   604	                        limit=limit,
   605	                        tier_filter=tier_filter,
   606	                        apply_filters=apply_filters,
   607	                    )
   608	                    if multi_result and multi_result.get("results"):
   609	                        return multi_result
   610	                except Exception as e:
   611	                    logger.warning("Multi-query expansion failed, falling back: %s", e)
   612

   614	            expanded_query = await self.query_expander.expand(query)
   615	            search_query = expanded_query  # Use expanded query for embedding
   616	
   617	            # Prepare search context (DRY: shared logic with search_with_reranking)
   618	            embedding_start = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
   619	            (
   620	                query_embedding,
   621	                collection_name,
   622	                vector_db,
   623	                chroma_filter,

   629	                collection_override,
   630	                apply_filters,

   634	
   635	            # Debug logging (only in debug mode)

   638	            )
   639	            logger.debug(

   641	                collection_override,

   646	            if chroma_filter:

   649	            # Try hybrid search if BM25 available
   650	            if self._bm25_enabled and self._bm25_vectorizer:

   652	                    # Generate sparse vector using BM25

   657	                        raw_results = await vector_db.hybrid_search(

   729	                if formatted_results
   730	                else 0.0
   731	            )
   732	            self.health_monitor.record_query(
   733	                collection_name=collection_name,

   738

   748	            logger.exception(

   760	            return {
   761	                "query": query,
   762	                "results": [],
   763	                "user_level": user_level,
   764	                "allowed_tiers": [],
   765	                "collection_used": collection_override or "unknown",
   766	                "error": "Search service temporarily unavailable",
